/**
   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
609 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
610 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,
611 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
612 |                                  SourceMap.DetailLevel sourceMapDetailLevel,
613 |                                  Charset outputCharset,
614 |                                  boolean tagAsStrict) {
615 |     Preconditions.checkState(sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
616 | 
617 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
618 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
619 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
620 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
621 |             lineBreak, lineLengthThreshold,
622 |             createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel)
623 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
624 |             lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel);
625 |     CodeGenerator cg =
626 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
627 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset)
628 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset);
629 | 
630 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
631 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
632 |     }
633 | 
634 |     cg.add(root);
635 |     mcp.endFile();
636 | 
637 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
638 | 
639 |     if (createSourceMap) {
640 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     return code;
644 |   }

===== M0004 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 234-277
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
234 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
235 |     this.type = type;
236 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
237 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
238 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
239 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
240 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
241 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
242 |       int i = 0;
243 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
244 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
245 |         i++;
246 |       }
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
250 | 
251 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
252 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
253 |       return;
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
257 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
258 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
259 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
260 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
261 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
262 |       trace.truncateEvents();
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
266 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
267 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
268 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
269 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
270 |                     + "try/finally block. "
271 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
272 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
273 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
274 |     }
275 | 
276 |     trace.startEvent(this);
277 |   }

===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 459-475
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
459 |     @Override
460 |     void maybeCutLine() {
461 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
462 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
463 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
464 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
465 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
466 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
467 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
468 |           lineIndex++;
469 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
470 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
471 |         } else {
472 |           startNewLine();
473 |         }
474 |       }
475 |     }

===== M0008 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.ContextualRenameInverter.exitScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 286-306
Documentation:
/**
     * Rename vars for the current scope, and merge any referenced
     * names into the parent scope reference set.
     */
Method code:
286 |     public void exitScope(NodeTraversal t) {
287 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
288 |         return;
289 |       }
290 | 
291 |       for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
292 |         Var v = it.next();
293 |         handleScopeVar(v);
294 |       }
295 | 
296 |       // Merge any names that were referenced but not declared in the current
297 |       // scope.
298 |       Set<String> current = referencedNames;
299 |       referencedNames = referenceStack.pop();
300 |       // If there isn't anything left in the stack we will be going into the
301 |       // global scope: don't try to build a set of referenced names for the
302 |       // global scope.
303 |       if (!referenceStack.isEmpty()) {
304 |         referencedNames.addAll(current);
305 |       }
306 |     }

380 |   private void validateVar(Node n) {
381 |     validateNodeType(Token.VAR, n);
382 |     this.validateMinimumChildCount(n, 1);
383 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
384 |       // Don't use the validateName here as the NAME is allowed to have
385 |       // a child.
386 |       validateNodeType(Token.NAME, c);
387 |       validateNonEmptyString(c);
388 |       validateMaximumChildCount(c, 1);
389 |       if (c.hasChildren()) {
390 |         validateExpression(c.getFirstChild());
391 |       }
392 |     }
393 |   }

===== M0014 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.collectAliasCandidates(NodeTraversal,ReferenceMap)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 169-190
Documentation:
/**
     * If any of the variables are well-defined and alias other variables,
     * mark them as aliasing candidates.
     */
Method code:
169 |     private void collectAliasCandidates(NodeTraversal t,
170 |         ReferenceMap referenceMap) {
171 |       if (mode != Mode.CONSTANTS_ONLY) {
172 |         for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
173 |           Var v = it.next();
174 |           ReferenceCollection referenceInfo = referenceMap.getReferences(v);
175 | 
176 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): Don't handle variables that are never used.
177 |           // The tests are much easier to write if you don't, and there's
178 |           // another pass that handles unused variables much more elegantly.
179 |           if (referenceInfo != null && referenceInfo.references.size() >= 2 &&
180 |               referenceInfo.isWellDefined() &&
181 |               referenceInfo.isAssignedOnceInLifetime()) {
182 |             Reference init = referenceInfo.getInitializingReference();
183 |             Node value = init.getAssignedValue();
184 |             if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.NAME) {
185 |               aliasCandidates.put(value, new AliasCandidate(v, referenceInfo));
186 |             }
187 |           }
188 |         }
189 |       }
190 |     }

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 118-149
Documentation:

Method code:
118 |   @Override
119 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
120 |     new NodeTraversal(
121 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
122 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
123 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
124 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
125 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
126 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
127 |     }
128 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
129 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
130 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
131 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
132 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
133 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
134 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
135 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
136 |     //   function f() {
137 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
138 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
139 |     //   }
140 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
141 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
142 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
143 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
144 |         .process(externs, root);
145 | 
146 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
147 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
148 |     }
149 |   }

===== M0039 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 682-779
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
682 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
683 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
684 |     /*
685 |      * This is the case where:
686 |      *
687 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
688 |      * the script.
689 |      *
690 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
691 |      * the caller of the function.
692 |      *
693 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
694 |      * back to the caller of the function.
695 |      *
696 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
697 |      * asked to traverse.
698 |      *
699 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
700 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
701 |      */
702 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
703 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
704 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
705 |       return null;
706 |     }
707 | 
708 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
709 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
710 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
711 |       case Token.IF:
712 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
713 |       case Token.CASE:
714 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
715 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
716 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
717 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
718 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
719 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
720 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
721 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
722 |           } else {
723 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
724 |           }
725 |         } else {
726 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
727 |         }
728 |         break;
729 |       case Token.FOR:
730 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
731 |           return parent;
732 |         } else {
733 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
734 |         }
735 |       case Token.WHILE:
736 |       case Token.DO:
737 |         return parent;
738 |       case Token.TRY:
739 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
740 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
743 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // CATCH block.
747 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
748 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
749 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
750 |           } else {
751 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
752 |           }
753 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
754 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
755 |           if (cfa != null) {
756 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
757 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
758 |             }
759 |           }
760 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
761 |         }
762 |     }
763 | 
764 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
765 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
766 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
767 | 
768 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
769 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
770 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
774 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
775 |     } else {
776 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
777 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
778 |     }
779 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.process(Node,Node)

Method code:
107 |   @Override
108 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
109 |     Preconditions.checkState(compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized());
110 | 
111 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, new FindCandidateFunctions());
112 |     if (fns.isEmpty()) {
113 |       return;  // Nothing left to do.
114 |     }
115 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
116 |        new FindCandidatesReferences(fns, anonFns));
117 |     trimCanidatesNotMeetingMinimumRequirements();
118 |     if (fns.isEmpty()) {
119 |       return;  // Nothing left to do.
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     // Store the set of function names eligible for inlining and use this to
123 |     // prevent function names from being moved into temporaries during
124 |     // expression decomposition. If this movement were allowed it would prevent
125 |     // the Inline callback from finding the function calls.
126 |     //
127 |     // This pass already assumes these are constants, so this is safe for anyone
128 |     // using function inlining.
129 |     //
130 |     Set<String> fnNames = Sets.newHashSet(fns.keySet());
131 |     injector.setKnownConstants(fnNames);
132 | 
133 |     trimCanidatesUsingOnCost();
134 |     if (fns.isEmpty()) {
135 |       return;  // Nothing left to do.
136 |     }
137 |     resolveInlineConflicts();
138 |     decomposeExpressions(fnNames);
139 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
140 |         new CallVisitor(
141 |             fns, anonFns, new Inline(injector, specializationState)));
142 | 
143 |     removeInlinedFunctions();
144 |   }

===== M0045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.doInlinesForScope(NodeTraversal,ReferenceMap)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 196-224
Documentation:
/**
     * For all variables in this scope, see if they are only used once.
     * If it looks safe to do so, inline them.
     */
Method code:
196 |     private void doInlinesForScope(NodeTraversal t, ReferenceMap referenceMap) {
197 | 
198 |       boolean maybeModifiedArguments =
199 |           maybeEscapedOrModifiedArguments(t.getScope(), referenceMap);
200 |       for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
201 |         Var v = it.next();
202 | 
203 |         ReferenceCollection referenceInfo = referenceMap.getReferences(v);
204 | 
205 |         // referenceInfo will be null if we're in constants-only mode
206 |         // and the variable is not a constant.
207 |         if (referenceInfo == null || isVarInlineForbidden(v)) {
208 |           // Never try to inline exported variables or variables that
209 |           // were not collected or variables that have already been inlined.
210 |           continue;
211 |         } else if (isInlineableDeclaredConstant(v, referenceInfo)) {
212 |           Reference init = referenceInfo.getInitializingReferenceForConstants();
213 |           Node value = init.getAssignedValue();
214 |           inlineDeclaredConstant(v, value, referenceInfo.references);
215 |           staleVars.add(v);
216 |         } else if (mode == Mode.CONSTANTS_ONLY) {
217 |           // If we're in constants-only mode, don't run more aggressive
218 |           // inlining heuristics. See InlineConstantsTest.
219 |           continue;
220 |         } else {
221 |           inlineNonConstants(v, referenceInfo, maybeModifiedArguments);
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |     }

===== M0048 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.tryReplaceIf(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 190-227
Documentation:
/**
   * Use "return x?1:2;" in place of "if(x)return 1;return 2;"
   */
Method code:
190 |   private Node tryReplaceIf(Node n) {
191 | 
192 |     for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
193 |          child != null; child = child.getNext()){
194 |       if (child.getType() == Token.IF){
195 | 
196 |         Node cond = child.getFirstChild();
197 |         Node thenBranch = cond.getNext();
198 |         Node elseBranch = thenBranch.getNext();
199 |         Node nextNode = child.getNext();
200 | 
201 |         if (nextNode != null && elseBranch == null &&
202 |             isReturnBlock(thenBranch) && isReturnExpression(nextNode)) {
203 |           Node thenExpr = null;
204 |           // if(x)return; return 1 -> return x?void 0:1
205 |           if (isReturnExpressBlock(thenBranch)) {
206 |             thenExpr = getBlockReturnExpression(thenBranch);
207 |             thenExpr.detachFromParent();
208 |           } else {
209 |             thenExpr = NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(child);
210 |           }
211 | 
212 |           Node elseExpr = nextNode.getFirstChild();
213 | 
214 |           cond.detachFromParent();
215 |           elseExpr.detachFromParent();
216 | 
217 |           Node hookNode = new Node(Token.HOOK, cond, thenExpr, elseExpr)
218 |                               .copyInformationFrom(child);
219 |           Node returnNode = new Node(Token.RETURN, hookNode);
220 |           n.replaceChild(child, returnNode);
221 |           n.removeChild(nextNode);
222 |           reportCodeChange();
223 |         }
224 |       }
225 |     }
226 |     return n;
227 |   }

===== M0065 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckAccidentalSemicolon.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckAccidentalSemicolon.java
Original range: 44-72
Documentation:

Method code:
44 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
45 |     Node child;
46 |     switch (n.getType()) {
47 |       case Token.IF:
48 |         child = n.getFirstChild().getNext();  // skip the condition child
49 |         break;
50 | 
51 |       case Token.WHILE:
52 |       case Token.FOR:
53 |         child = NodeUtil.getLoopCodeBlock(n);
54 |         break;
55 | 
56 |       default:
57 |         return;  // don't check other types
58 |     }
59 | 
60 |     // semicolons cause VOID children. Empty blocks are allowed because
61 |     // that's usually intentional, especially with loops.
62 |     for (; child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
63 |       if ((child.getType() == Token.BLOCK) && (!child.hasChildren())) {
64 |         // Only warn on empty blocks that replaced EMPTY nodes.  BLOCKs with no
65 |         // children are considered OK.
66 |         if (child.wasEmptyNode()) {
67 |           t.getCompiler().report(
68 |               t.makeError(n, level, SUSPICIOUS_SEMICOLON));
69 |         }
70 |       }
71 |     }
72 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)

Original range: 187-204

/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
187 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
188 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
189 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
190 |     if (parent == null
191 |         || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION
192 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
193 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
194 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
195 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
196 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
197 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
198 |       }
199 | 
200 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
201 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
202 |       }
203 |     }
204 |   }

===== M0075 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CoalesceVariableNames.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CoalesceVariableNames.java
Original range: 134-197
Documentation:

Method code:
134 |   @Override
135 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
136 |     if (colorings.isEmpty() || !NodeUtil.isName(n) ||
137 |         NodeUtil.isFunction(parent)) {
138 |       // Don't rename named functions.
139 |       return;
140 |     }
141 |     Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
142 |     GraphNode<Var, ?> vNode = colorings.peek().getGraph().getNode(var);
143 |     if (vNode == null) {
144 |       // This is not a local.
145 |       return;
146 |     }
147 |     Var coalescedVar = colorings.peek().getPartitionSuperNode(var);
148 | 
149 |     if (!usePseudoNames) {
150 |       if (vNode.getValue().equals(coalescedVar)) {
151 |         // The coalesced name is itself, nothing to do.
152 |         return;
153 |       }
154 | 
155 |       // Rename.
156 |       n.setString(coalescedVar.name);
157 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
158 | 
159 |       if (NodeUtil.isVar(parent)) {
160 |         removeVarDeclaration(n);
161 |       }
162 |     } else {
163 |       // This code block is slow but since usePseudoName is for debugging,
164 |       // we should not sacrifice performance for non-debugging compilation to
165 |       // make this fast.
166 |       String pseudoName = null;
167 |       Set<String> allMergedNames = Sets.newTreeSet();
168 |       for (Iterator<Var> i = t.getScope().getVars(); i.hasNext();) {
169 |         Var iVar = i.next();
170 | 
171 |         // Look for all the variables that can be merged (in the graph by now)
172 |         // and it is merged with the current coalscedVar.
173 |         if (colorings.peek().getGraph().getNode(iVar) != null &&
174 |             coalescedVar.equals(colorings.peek().getPartitionSuperNode(iVar))) {
175 |           allMergedNames.add(iVar.name);
176 |         }
177 |       }
178 | 
179 |       // Keep its original name.
180 |       if (allMergedNames.size() == 1) {
181 |         return;
182 |       }
183 | 
184 |       pseudoName = Joiner.on("_").join(allMergedNames);
185 | 
186 |       while (t.getScope().isDeclared(pseudoName, true)) {
187 |         pseudoName += "$";
188 |       }
189 | 
190 |       n.setString(pseudoName);
191 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
192 | 
193 |       if (!vNode.getValue().equals(coalescedVar) && NodeUtil.isVar(parent)) {
194 |         removeVarDeclaration(n);
195 |       }
196 |     }
197 |   }

===== M0077 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckUnreachableCode.ReachablePredicate.apply(EdgeTuple<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckUnreachableCode.java
Original range: 94-112
Documentation:

Method code:
94 |     @Override
95 |     public boolean apply(EdgeTuple<Node, Branch> input) {
96 |       Branch branch = input.edge;
97 |       if (!branch.isConditional()) {
98 |         return true;
99 |       }
100 |       Node predecessor = input.sourceNode;
101 |       Node condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(predecessor);
102 | 
103 |       // TODO(user): Handle more complicated expression like true == true,
104 |       // etc....
105 |       if (condition != null) {
106 |         TernaryValue val = NodeUtil.getImpureBooleanValue(condition);
107 |         if (val != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
108 |           return val.toBoolean(true) == (branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
109 |         }
110 |       }
111 |       return true;
112 |     }

===== M0085 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 141-167
Documentation:

Method code:
141 |   @Override
142 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
143 |     switch (n.getType()) {
144 |       case Token.NAME:
145 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
146 |         if (newName != null) {
147 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
148 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
149 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the javadoc?
150 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
151 |           }
152 |           n.setString(newName);
153 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
154 |         }
155 |         break;
156 | 
157 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
158 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
159 |         nameStack.pop();
160 |         break;
161 | 
162 |       case Token.CATCH:
163 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
164 |         nameStack.pop();
165 |         break;
166 |     }

===== M0088 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.traverseNode(Node,Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 190-294
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses everything in the current scope and marks variables that
   * are referenced.
   *
   * During traversal, we identify subtrees that will only be
   * referenced if their enclosing variables are referenced. Instead of
   * traversing those subtrees, we create a continuation for them,
   * and traverse them lazily.
   */
Method code:
190 |   private void traverseNode(Node n, Node parent, Scope scope) {
191 |     int type = n.getType();
192 |     Var var = null;
193 |     switch (type) {
194 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
195 |         // If this function is a removable var, then create a continuation
196 |         // for it instead of traversing immediately.
197 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
198 |           var = scope.getVar(n.getFirstChild().getString());
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (var != null && isRemovableVar(var)) {
202 |           continuations.put(var, new Continuation(n, scope));
203 |         } else {
204 |           traverseFunction(n, scope);
205 |         }
206 |         return;
207 | 
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
209 |         Assign maybeAssign = Assign.maybeCreateAssign(n);
210 |         if (maybeAssign != null) {
211 |           // Put this in the assign map. It might count as a reference,
212 |           // but we won't know that until we have an index of all assigns.
213 |           var = scope.getVar(maybeAssign.nameNode.getString());
214 |           if (var != null) {
215 |             assignsByVar.put(var, maybeAssign);
216 |             assignsByNode.put(maybeAssign.nameNode, maybeAssign);
217 | 
218 |             if (isRemovableVar(var) &&
219 |                 !maybeAssign.mayHaveSecondarySideEffects) {
220 |               // If the var is unreferenced and performing this assign has
221 |               // no secondary side effects, then we can create a continuation
222 |               // for it instead of traversing immediately.
223 |               continuations.put(var, new Continuation(n, scope));
224 |               return;
225 |             }
226 |           }
227 |         }
228 |         break;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.CALL:
231 |         // Look for calls to inheritance-defining calls (such as goog.inherits).
232 |         SubclassRelationship subclassRelationship =
233 |             codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
234 |         if (subclassRelationship != null) {
235 |           Var subclassVar = scope.getVar(subclassRelationship.subclassName);
236 |           // Don't try to track the inheritance calls for non-globals. It would
237 |           // be more correct to only not track when the subclass does not
238 |           // reference a constructor, but checking that it is a global is
239 |           // easier and mostly the same.
240 |           if (subclassVar != null && subclassVar.isGlobal()
241 |               && !referenced.contains(subclassVar)) {
242 |             // Save a reference to the EXPR node.
243 |             inheritsCalls.put(subclassVar, parent);
244 |             continuations.put(subclassVar, new Continuation(n, scope));
245 |             return;
246 |           }
247 |         }
248 |         break;
249 | 
250 |       case Token.NAME:
251 |         var = scope.getVar(n.getString());
252 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
253 |           Node value = n.getFirstChild();
254 |           if (value != null && var != null && isRemovableVar(var) &&
255 |               !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(value)) {
256 |             // If the var is unreferenced and creating its value has no side
257 |             // effects, then we can create a continuation for it instead
258 |             // of traversing immediately.
259 |             continuations.put(var, new Continuation(n, scope));
260 |             return;
261 |           }
262 |         } else {
263 | 
264 |           // If arguments is escaped, we just assume the worst and continue
265 |           // on all the parameters.
266 |           if ("arguments".equals(n.getString()) && scope.isLocal()) {
267 |             Node lp = scope.getRootNode().getFirstChild().getNext();
268 |             for (Node a = lp.getFirstChild(); a != null; a = a.getNext()) {
269 |               markReferencedVar(scope.getVar(a.getString()));
270 |             }
271 |           }
272 | 
273 |           // All name references that aren't declarations or assigns
274 |           // are references to other vars.
275 |           if (var != null) {
276 |             // If that var hasn't already been marked referenced, then
277 |             // start tracking it.  If this is an assign, do nothing
278 |             // for now.
279 |             if (isRemovableVar(var)) {
280 |               if (!assignsByNode.containsKey(n)) {
281 |                 markReferencedVar(var);
282 |               }
283 |             } else {
284 |               markReferencedVar(var);
285 |             }
286 |           }
287 |         }
288 |         break;
289 |     }
290 | 
291 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
292 |       traverseNode(c, n, scope);
293 |     }
294 |   }

881 |         sourceMap, externExports, state.cssNames, state.idGeneratorMap);

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.clearCachedValues()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 937-956
Documentation:

Method code:
937 |   @Override
938 |   public void clearCachedValues() {
939 |     super.clearCachedValues();
940 | 
941 |     if (subTypes != null) {
942 |       for (FunctionType subType : subTypes) {
943 |         subType.clearCachedValues();
944 |       }
945 |     }
946 | 
947 |     if (!isNativeObjectType()) {
948 |       if (hasInstanceType()) {
949 |         getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
950 |       }
951 | 
952 |       if (prototype != null) {
953 |         prototype.clearCachedValues();
954 |       }
955 |     }
956 |   }

   * @param item The item to inspect

776 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
777 |     if (item == null) {

===== M0097 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.handleSetFromGlobal(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String,boolean,Name.Type)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 492-513
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect an
     * assignment to a global name in global scope.
     *
     * @param t The traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @param isPropAssign Whether this set corresponds to a property
     *     assignment of the form <code>a.b.c = ...;</code>
     * @param type The type of the value that the name is being assigned
     */
Method code:
492 |     void handleSetFromGlobal(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent, String name,
493 |                              boolean isPropAssign, Name.Type type) {
494 |       if (maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(t, n, parent, name)) return;
495 | 
496 |       Name nameObj = getOrCreateName(name);
497 |       nameObj.type = type;
498 | 
499 |       Ref set = new Ref(t, n, Ref.Type.SET_FROM_GLOBAL,
500 |           currentPreOrderIndex++);
501 |       nameObj.addRef(set);
502 | 
503 |       if (isNestedAssign(parent)) {
504 |         // This assignment is both a set and a get that creates an alias.
505 |         Ref get = new Ref(t, n, Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET,
506 |             currentPreOrderIndex++);
507 |         nameObj.addRef(get);
508 |         Ref.markTwins(set, get);
509 |       } else if (isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(n, parent)) {
510 |         // Names with a @constructor or @enum annotation are always collapsed
511 |         nameObj.setIsClassOrEnum();
512 |       }
513 |     }

===== M0112 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckSideEffects.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckSideEffects.java
Original range: 47-118
Documentation:

Method code:
47 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
48 |     // VOID nodes appear when there are extra semicolons at the BLOCK level.
49 |     // I've been unable to think of any cases where this indicates a bug,
50 |     // and apparently some people like keeping these semicolons around,
51 |     // so we'll allow it.
52 |     if (n.getType() == Token.EMPTY ||
53 |         n.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
54 |       return;
55 |     }
56 | 
57 |     if (parent == null)
58 |       return;
59 | 
60 |     int pt = parent.getType();
61 |     if (pt == Token.COMMA) {
62 |       Node gramps = parent.getParent();
63 |       if (gramps.getType() == Token.CALL &&
64 |           parent == gramps.getFirstChild()) {
65 |         // Semantically, a direct call to eval is different from an indirect
66 |         // call to an eval. See Ecma-262 S15.1.2.1. So it's ok for the first
67 |         // expression to a comma to be a no-op if it's used to indirect
68 |         // an eval.
69 |         if (n == parent.getFirstChild() &&
70 |             parent.getChildCount() == 2 &&
71 |             n.getNext().getType() == Token.NAME &&
72 |             "eval".equals(n.getNext().getString())) {
73 |           return;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       if (n == parent.getLastChild()) {
78 |         for (Node an : parent.getAncestors()) {
79 |           int ancestorType = an.getType();
80 |           if (ancestorType == Token.COMMA)
81 |             continue;
82 |           if (ancestorType != Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
83 |               ancestorType != Token.BLOCK)
84 |             return;
85 |           else
86 |             break;
87 |         }
88 |       }
89 |     } else if (pt != Token.EXPR_RESULT && pt != Token.BLOCK) {
90 |       if (pt == Token.FOR && parent.getChildCount() == 4 &&
91 |           (n == parent.getFirstChild() ||
92 |            n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext())) {
93 |         // Fall through and look for warnings for the 1st and 3rd child
94 |         // of a for.
95 |       } else {
96 |         return;  // it might be ok to not have a side-effect
97 |       }
98 |     }
99 |     if (NodeUtil.isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType()) ||
100 |         !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(n, t.getCompiler())) {
101 |       if (n.isQualifiedName() && n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
102 |         // This no-op statement was there so that JSDoc information could
103 |         // be attached to the name. This check should not complain about it.
104 |         return;
105 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(n)) {
106 |         // we already reported the problem when we visited the child.
107 |         return;
108 |       }
109 | 
110 |       String msg = "This code lacks side-effects. Is there a bug?";
111 |       if (n.getType() == Token.STRING) {
112 |         msg = "Is there a missing '+' on the previous line?";
113 |       }
114 | 
115 |       t.getCompiler().report(
116 |           t.makeError(n, level, USELESS_CODE_ERROR, msg));
117 |     }
118 |   }

149 |   @Override
150 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
151 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(traversal);

153 | 
154 |

===== M0124 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.compileInternal()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 625-665
Documentation:

Method code:
625 |   private void compileInternal() {
626 |     parse();
627 |     if (hasErrors()) {
628 |       return;
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     if (!precheck()) {
632 |       return;
633 |     }
634 | 
635 |     if (options.nameAnonymousFunctionsOnly) {
636 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Move this into an instrument() phase maybe?
637 |       check();
638 |       return;
639 |     }
640 | 
641 |     if (!options.skipAllPasses) {
642 |       check();
643 |       if (hasErrors()) {
644 |         return;
645 |       }
646 | 
647 |       if (options.isExternExportsEnabled()
648 |           || options.externExportsPath != null) {
649 |         externExports();

664 |     }

===== M0126 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseVariableDeclarations.GatherCollapses.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseVariableDeclarations.java
Original range: 135-177
Documentation:

Method code:
135 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
136 |       if (n.getType() == Token.VAR) {
137 |         blacklistStubVars(t, n);
138 |       }
139 | 
140 |       // Only care about var nodes
141 |       if (n.getType() != Token.VAR && !canBeRedeclared(n, t.getScope())) return;
142 | 
143 |       // If we've already looked at this node, skip it
144 |       if (nodesToCollapse.contains(n)) return;
145 | 
146 |       // Adjacent VAR children of an IF node are the if and else parts and can't
147 |       // be collapsed
148 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.IF) return;
149 | 
150 |       Node varNode = n;
151 | 
152 |       boolean hasVar = n.getType() == Token.VAR;
153 | 
154 |       // Find variable declarations that follow this one (if any)
155 |       n = n.getNext();
156 | 
157 |       boolean hasNodesToCollapse = false;
158 | 
159 |       while (n != null &&
160 |           (n.getType() == Token.VAR || canBeRedeclared(n, t.getScope()))) {
161 | 
162 |         if (NodeUtil.isVar(n)) {
163 |           blacklistStubVars(t, n);
164 |           hasVar = true;
165 |         }
166 | 
167 |         nodesToCollapse.add(n);
168 |         hasNodesToCollapse = true;
169 | 
170 |         n = n.getNext();
171 |       }
172 | 
173 |       if (hasNodesToCollapse && hasVar) {
174 |         nodesToCollapse.add(varNode);
175 |         collapses.add(new Collapse(varNode, n, parent));
176 |       }
177 |     }

===== M0135 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.FixedPointGraphTraversal.computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E>,Set<N>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/FixedPointGraphTraversal.java
Original range: 93-128
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute a fixed point for the given graph, entering from the given nodes.
   * @param graph The graph to traverse.
   * @param entrySet The nodes to begin traversing from.
   */
Method code:
93 |   public void computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E> graph, Set<N> entrySet) {
94 |     int cycleCount = 0;
95 |     long nodeCount = graph.getNodes().size();
96 | 
97 |     // Choose a bail-out heuristically in case the computation
98 |     // doesn't converge.
99 |     long maxIterations = Math.max(nodeCount * nodeCount * nodeCount, 100);
100 | 
101 |     // Use a LinkedHashSet, so that the traversal is deterministic.
102 |     LinkedHashSet<DiGraphNode<N, E>> workSet =
103 |         Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
104 |     for (N n : entrySet) {
105 |       workSet.add(graph.getDirectedGraphNode(n));
106 |     }
107 |     for (; !workSet.isEmpty() && cycleCount < maxIterations; cycleCount++) {
108 |       // For every out edge in the workSet, traverse that edge. If that
109 |       // edge updates the state of the graph, then add the destination
110 |       // node to the resultSet, so that we can update all of its out edges
111 |       // on the next iteration.
112 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> source = workSet.iterator().next();
113 |       N sourceValue = source.getValue();
114 | 
115 |       workSet.remove(source);
116 | 
117 |       List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> outEdges = source.getOutEdges();
118 |       for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> edge : outEdges) {
119 |         N destNode = edge.getDestination().getValue();
120 |         if (callback.traverseEdge(sourceValue, edge.getValue(), destNode)) {
121 |           workSet.add(edge.getDestination());
122 |         }
123 |       }
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     Preconditions.checkState(cycleCount != maxIterations,
127 |         NON_HALTING_ERROR_MSG);
128 |   }

317 |     @Override
318 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
319 |       switch (n.getType()) {
320 |         case Token.WHILE:
321 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
322 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
323 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
324 |             Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);

334 |           break;

Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureOptimizePrimitives.processObjectCreateCalls(List<Node>)

Original range: 74-100
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts all of the given call nodes to object literals that are safe to
   * do so.
   */
Method code:
74 |   private void processObjectCreateCalls(List<Node> callNodes) {
75 |     for (Node callNode : callNodes) {
76 |       Node curParam = callNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
77 |       if (canOptimizeObjectCreate(curParam)) {
78 |         Node objNode = new Node(Token.OBJECTLIT)
79 |             .copyInformationFrom(callNode);
80 |         while (curParam != null) {
81 |           Node keyNode = curParam;
82 |           Node valueNode = curParam.getNext();
83 |           curParam = valueNode.getNext();
84 | 
85 |           callNode.removeChild(keyNode);
86 |           callNode.removeChild(valueNode);
87 | 
88 |           if (keyNode.getType() != Token.STRING) {
89 |             keyNode = Node.newString(NodeUtil.getStringValue(keyNode))
90 |                 .copyInformationFrom(keyNode);
91 |           }
92 |           keyNode.setQuotedString();
93 |           keyNode.addChildToBack(valueNode);
94 |           objNode.addChildToBack(keyNode);
95 |         }
96 |         callNode.getParent().replaceChild(callNode, objNode);
97 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
98 |       }
99 |     }
100 |   }

===== M0170 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 122-150
Documentation:

Method code:
122 |   private void overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo> allDefines) {
123 |     boolean changed = false;
124 |     for (Map.Entry<String, DefineInfo> def : allDefines.entrySet()) {
125 |       String defineName = def.getKey();
126 |       DefineInfo info = def.getValue();
127 |       Node inputValue = dominantReplacements.get(defineName);
128 |       Node finalValue = inputValue != null ?
129 |           inputValue : info.getLastValue();
130 |       if (finalValue != info.initialValue) {
131 |         info.initialValueParent.replaceChild(
132 |             info.initialValue, finalValue.cloneTree());
133 |         compiler.addToDebugLog("Overriding @define variable " + defineName);
134 |         changed = changed ||
135 |             finalValue.getType() != info.initialValue.getType() ||
136 |             !finalValue.isEquivalentTo(info.initialValue);
137 |       }
138 |     }
139 | 
140 |     if (changed) {
141 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
142 |     }
143 | 
144 |     Set<String> unusedReplacements = dominantReplacements.keySet();
145 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(allDefines.keySet());
146 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(KNOWN_DEFINES);
147 |     for (String unknownDefine : unusedReplacements) {
148 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(UNKNOWN_DEFINE_WARNING, unknownDefine));
149 |     }
150 |   }

/**

   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *

   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1077 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {

1081 | 
1082 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1083 |     int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1084 | 
1085 |     jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endCharno,
1086 |         true);
1087 |     return typeNode;
1088 |   }

===== M0178 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

===== M0185 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.processResults(Result,List<JSModule>,B)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 669-785
Documentation:
/**
   * Processes the results of the compile job, and returns an error code.
   */
Method code:
669 |   int processResults(Result result, List<JSModule> modules, B options)
670 |        throws FlagUsageException, IOException {
671 |     if (config.computePhaseOrdering) {
672 |       return 0;
673 |     }
674 | 
675 |     if (config.printPassGraph) {
676 |       if (compiler.getRoot() == null) {
677 |         return 1;
678 |       } else {
679 |         jsOutput.append(
680 |             DotFormatter.toDot(compiler.getPassConfig().getPassGraph()));
681 |         jsOutput.append('\n');
682 |         return 0;
683 |       }
684 |     }
685 | 
686 |     if (config.printAst) {
687 |       if (compiler.getRoot() == null) {
688 |         return 1;
689 |       } else {
690 |         ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg = compiler.computeCFG();
691 |         DotFormatter.appendDot(
692 |             compiler.getRoot().getLastChild(), cfg, jsOutput);
693 |         jsOutput.append('\n');
694 |         return 0;
695 |       }
696 |     }
697 | 
698 |     if (config.printTree) {
699 |       if (compiler.getRoot() == null) {
700 |         jsOutput.append("Code contains errors; no tree was generated.\n");
701 |         return 1;
702 |       } else {
703 |         compiler.getRoot().appendStringTree(jsOutput);
704 |         jsOutput.append("\n");
705 |         return 0;
706 |       }
707 |     }
708 | 
709 |     if (config.skipNormalOutputs) {
710 |       // Output the manifest if requested.
711 |       outputManifest();
712 |       return 0;
713 |     } else if (result.success) {
714 |       if (modules == null) {
715 |         writeOutput(
716 |             jsOutput, compiler, compiler.toSource(), config.outputWrapper,
717 |             OUTPUT_WRAPPER_MARKER);
718 | 
719 |         // Output the source map if requested.
720 |         outputSourceMap(options);
721 |       } else {
722 |         String moduleFilePrefix = config.moduleOutputPathPrefix;
723 |         maybeCreateDirsForPath(moduleFilePrefix);
724 |         Map<String, String> moduleWrappers =
725 |             parseModuleWrappers(config.moduleWrapper, modules);
726 | 
727 |         // If the source map path is in fact a pattern for each
728 |         // module, create a stream per-module. Otherwise, create
729 |         // a single source map.
730 |         Writer mapOut = null;
731 | 
732 |         if (!shouldGenerateMapPerModule(options)) {
733 |           mapOut = fileNameToOutputWriter(expandSourceMapPath(options, null));
734 |         }
735 | 
736 |         for (JSModule m : modules) {
737 |           if (shouldGenerateMapPerModule(options)) {
738 |             mapOut = fileNameToOutputWriter(expandSourceMapPath(options, m));
739 |           }
740 | 
741 |           Writer writer = fileNameToOutputWriter(
742 |               moduleFilePrefix + m.getName() + ".js");
743 | 
744 |           if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null) {
745 |             compiler.getSourceMap().reset();
746 |           }
747 | 
748 |           writeOutput(writer, compiler, compiler.toSource(m),
749 |               moduleWrappers.get(m.getName()), "%s");
750 | 
751 |           if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null) {
752 |             compiler.getSourceMap().appendTo(mapOut, m.getName());
753 |           }
754 | 
755 |           writer.close();
756 | 
757 |           if (shouldGenerateMapPerModule(options) && mapOut != null) {
758 |             mapOut.close();
759 |             mapOut = null;
760 |           }
761 |         }
762 | 
763 |         if (mapOut != null) {
764 |           mapOut.close();
765 |         }
766 |       }
767 | 
768 |       // Output the externs if required.
769 |       if (options.externExportsPath != null) {
770 |         Writer eeOut =
771 |             openExternExportsStream(options, options.jsOutputFile);
772 |         eeOut.append(result.externExport);
773 |         eeOut.close();
774 |       }
775 | 
776 |       // Output the variable and property name maps if requested.
777 |       outputNameMaps(options);
778 | 
779 |       // Output the manifest if requested.
780 |       outputManifest();
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     // return 0 if no errors, the error count otherwise
784 |     return Math.min(result.errors.length, 0x7f);
785 |   }

===== M0199 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.tryOptimizeConditionalAfterAssign(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.java
Original range: 480-521
Documentation:
/**
   * Attempt to replace the condition of if or hook immediately that is a
   * reference to a name that is assigned immediately before.
   */
Method code:
480 |   private void tryOptimizeConditionalAfterAssign(Node n) {
481 |     Node next = n.getNext();
482 | 
483 |     // Look for patterns like the following and replace the if-condition with
484 |     // a constant value so it can later be folded:
485 |     //   var a = /a/;
486 |     //   if (a) {foo(a)}
487 |     // or
488 |     //   a = 0;
489 |     //   a ? foo(a) : c;
490 |     // or
491 |     //   a = 0;
492 |     //   a || foo(a);
493 |     // or
494 |     //   a = 0;
495 |     //   a && foo(a)
496 |     //
497 |     // TODO(johnlenz): This would be better handled by control-flow sensitive
498 |     // constant propagation. As the other case that I want to handle is:
499 |     //   i=0; for(;i<0;i++){}
500 |     // as right now nothing facilitates removing a loop like that.
501 |     // This is here simply to remove the cruft left behind goog.userAgent and
502 |     // similar cases.
503 | 
504 |     if (isSimpleAssignment(n) && isConditionalStatement(next)) {
505 |       Node lhsAssign = getSimpleAssignmentName(n);
506 | 
507 |       Node condition = getConditionalStatementCondition(next);
508 |       if (NodeUtil.isName(lhsAssign) && NodeUtil.isName(condition)
509 |           && lhsAssign.getString().equals(condition.getString())) {
510 |         Node rhsAssign = getSimpleAssignmentValue(n);
511 |         TernaryValue value = NodeUtil.getImpureBooleanValue(rhsAssign);
512 |         if (value != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
513 |           int replacementConditionNodeType =
514 |             (value.toBoolean(true)) ? Token.TRUE : Token.FALSE;
515 |           condition.getParent().replaceChild(condition,
516 |               new Node(replacementConditionNodeType));
517 |           reportCodeChange();
518 |         }
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 226-282
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
226 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
227 |     this.options = options;
228 |     if (errorManager == null) {
229 |       if (outStream == null) {
230 |         setErrorManager(
231 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
232 |       } else {
233 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
234 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
235 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
236 |         setErrorManager(printer);
237 |       }
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
241 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
242 |       options.checkTypes = true;
243 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
244 |       options.checkTypes = false;
245 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
246 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
247 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
248 |       // parser type warnings.
249 |       options.setWarningLevel(
250 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
251 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
252 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
253 |     }
254 | 
255 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn()) {
256 |       options.setWarningLevel(
257 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
258 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
259 |     }
260 | 
261 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
262 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
263 |     guards.add(
264 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
265 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
266 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
267 | 
268 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
269 | 
270 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
271 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
272 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
273 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
274 |     // error off.
275 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
276 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
277 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
278 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
282 |   }

===== M0213 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1091-1229
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1091 |   Node parseInputs() {
1092 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1093 | 
1094 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1095 |     // individual file parse trees.
1096 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1097 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1098 |     }
1099 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1100 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1101 |     }
1102 | 
1103 |     // Parse main js sources.
1104 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1105 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1106 | 
1107 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1108 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1109 | 
1110 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1111 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1112 | 
1113 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1114 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1115 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1116 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1117 |     }
1118 | 
1119 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1120 | 
1121 |     try {
1122 |       // Parse externs sources.
1123 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1124 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1125 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1126 |           return null;
1127 |         }
1128 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1129 |       }
1130 | 
1131 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1132 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1133 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1134 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1135 | 
1136 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1137 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1138 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1139 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1140 |           }
1141 |         }
1142 | 
1143 |         try {
1144 |           inputs =
1145 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1146 |               .manageDependencies(
1147 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1148 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1149 |           report(JSError.make(
1150 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1151 |           return null;
1152 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1153 |           report(JSError.make(
1154 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1155 |           return null;
1156 |         }
1157 |       }
1158 | 
1159 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1160 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1161 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1162 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1163 | 
1164 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1165 |         if (n == null) {
1166 |           continue;
1167 |         }
1168 | 
1169 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1170 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1171 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1172 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1173 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1174 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1175 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1176 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1177 | 
1178 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1179 | 
1180 |             externs.add(input);
1181 |             staleInputs = true;
1182 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1183 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1184 |             staleInputs = true;
1185 |           }
1186 |         }
1187 |       }
1188 | 
1189 |       if (staleInputs) {
1190 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1191 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1192 |       }
1193 | 
1194 |       // Build the AST.
1195 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1196 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1197 |         if (n == null) {
1198 |           continue;
1199 |         }
1200 | 
1201 |         if (devMode) {
1202 |           runSanityCheck();
1203 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1204 |             return null;
1205 |           }
1206 |         }
1207 | 
1208 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1209 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1210 | 
1211 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1212 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1213 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1214 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1215 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1216 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1217 |         }
1218 | 
1219 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1220 |       }
1221 | 
1222 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1223 |         return null;
1224 |       }
1225 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1226 |     } finally {
1227 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1228 |     }
1229 |   }

===== M0224 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MinimizeExitPoints.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MinimizeExitPoints.java
Original range: 50-83
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   @Override
51 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
52 |     switch (n.getType()) {
53 |       case Token.LABEL:
54 |         tryMinimizeExits(
55 |             n.getLastChild(), Token.BREAK, n.getFirstChild().getString());
56 |         break;
57 | 
58 |       case Token.FOR:
59 |       case Token.WHILE:
60 |         tryMinimizeExits(
61 |             NodeUtil.getLoopCodeBlock(n), Token.CONTINUE, null);
62 |         break;
63 | 
64 |       case Token.DO:
65 |         tryMinimizeExits(
66 |             NodeUtil.getLoopCodeBlock(n), Token.CONTINUE, null);
67 | 
68 |         Node cond = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n);
69 |         if (NodeUtil.getImpureBooleanValue(cond) == TernaryValue.FALSE) {
70 |           // Normally, we wouldn't be able to optimize BREAKs inside a loop
71 |           // but as we know the condition will always false, we can treat them
72 |           // as we would a CONTINUE.
73 |           tryMinimizeExits(
74 |               n.getFirstChild(), Token.BREAK, null);
75 |         }
76 |         break;
77 | 
78 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
79 |         tryMinimizeExits(
80 |             n.getLastChild(), Token.RETURN, null);
81 |         break;
82 |     }
83 |   }

/** Validates a name prefix. */
Method code:
90 |   private void checkPrefix(String prefix) {
91 |     if (prefix.length() > 0) {
92 |       // Make sure that prefix starts with a legal character.
93 |       if (!contains(firstChars, prefix.charAt(0))) {
94 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("prefix must start with one of: " +
95 |                                            Arrays.toString(firstChars));
96 |       }
97 |       for (int pos = 1; pos < prefix.length(); ++pos) {
98 |         if (!contains(nonFirstChars, prefix.charAt(pos))) {
99 |           throw new IllegalArgumentException("prefix has invalid characters, " +
100 |                                              "must be one of: " +
101 |                                              Arrays.toString(nonFirstChars));
102 |         }
103 |       }
104 |     }
105 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {
173 |       int size = alternates.size();
174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |   }

===== M0263 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ShadowVariables.GatherReferenceInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ShadowVariables.java
Original range: 129-169
Documentation:

Method code:
129 |     @Override
130 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
131 |       // Skipping over non-name nodes and empty function names.
132 |       if (!NodeUtil.isReferenceName(n)) {
133 |         return;
134 |       }
135 | 
136 |       // We focus on shadowing local variables as their name occurs much more
137 |       // than global names.
138 |       // TODO(user): Alternatively, we could experiment with using a local
139 |       // name to shadow a global variable.
140 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
141 |         return;
142 |       }
143 | 
144 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
145 |       if (var == null) {
146 |         // extern name or undefined name.
147 |         return;
148 |       }
149 | 
150 |       if (var.getScope().isGlobal()) {
151 |         // We will not shadow a global variable name.
152 |         return;
153 |       }
154 | 
155 |       // Using the definition of upward referencing, fill in the map.
156 |       if (var.getScope() != t.getScope()) {
157 |         for (Scope s = t.getScope();
158 |             s != var.getScope() && s.isLocal(); s = s.getParent()) {
159 |           scopeUpRefMap.put(s.getRootNode(), var.name);
160 |         }
161 |       }
162 | 
163 |       if (var.getScope() == t.getScope()) {
164 |         scopeUpRefMap.put(t.getScopeRoot(), var.name);
165 |       }
166 | 
167 |       // Find in the usage map that tracks a var and all of its usage.
168 |       varToNameUsage.put(var, n);
169 |     }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1283-1303
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts the main parse tree back to js code.
   */
Method code:
1283 |   public String toSource() {
1284 |     return runInCompilerThread(new Callable<String>() {
1285 |       public String call() throws Exception {
1286 |         Tracer tracer = newTracer("toSource");
1287 |         try {
1288 |           CodeBuilder cb = new CodeBuilder();
1289 |           if (jsRoot != null) {
1290 |             int i = 0;
1291 |             for (Node scriptNode = jsRoot.getFirstChild();
1292 |                  scriptNode != null;
1293 |                  scriptNode = scriptNode.getNext()) {
1294 |               toSource(cb, i++, scriptNode);
1295 |             }
1296 |           }
1297 |           return cb.toString();
1298 |         } finally {
1299 |           stopTracer(tracer, "toSource");
1300 |         }
1301 |       }
1302 |     });
1303 |   }

===== M0272 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.doRun()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 618-664
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses command-line arguments and runs the compiler.
   *
   * @return system exit status
   */
Method code:
618 |   protected int doRun() throws FlagUsageException, IOException {
619 |     Compiler.setLoggingLevel(Level.parse(config.loggingLevel));
620 | 
621 |     List<JSSourceFile> externs = createExterns();
622 | 
623 |     compiler = createCompiler();
624 |     B options = createOptions();
625 | 
626 |     List<JSModule> modules = null;
627 |     Result result = null;
628 | 
629 |     setRunOptions(options);
630 | 
631 |     boolean writeOutputToFile = !options.jsOutputFile.isEmpty();
632 |     if (writeOutputToFile) {
633 |       jsOutput = fileNameToOutputWriter(options.jsOutputFile);
634 |     } else if (jsOutput instanceof OutputStream) {
635 |       jsOutput = streamToOutputWriter((OutputStream) jsOutput);
636 |     }
637 | 
638 |     List<String> jsFiles = config.js;
639 |     List<String> moduleSpecs = config.module;
640 |     if (!moduleSpecs.isEmpty()) {
641 |       modules = createJsModules(moduleSpecs, jsFiles);
642 |       if (config.skipNormalOutputs) {
643 |         compiler.initModules(externs, modules, options);
644 |       } else {
645 |         result = compiler.compileModules(externs, modules, options);
646 |       }
647 |     } else {
648 |       List<JSSourceFile> inputs = createSourceInputs(jsFiles);
649 |       if (config.skipNormalOutputs) {
650 |         compiler.init(externs, inputs, options);
651 |       } else {
652 |         result = compiler.compile(externs, inputs, options);
653 |       }
654 |     }
655 | 
656 |     int errCode = processResults(result, modules, options);
657 |     // Flush the output if we are writing to a file.
658 |     // We can't close yet, because we may need to write phase ordering
659 |     // info to it later.
660 |     if (jsOutput instanceof Flushable) {
661 |       ((Flushable) jsOutput).flush();
662 |     }
663 |     return errCode;
664 |   }

900 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.outputManifest()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 1152-1185
Documentation:
/**
   * Writes the manifest of all compiler input files that survived
   * manage_closure_dependencies, if requested.
   */
Method code:
1152 |   private void outputManifest() throws IOException {
1153 |     List<String> outputManifests = config.outputManifests;
1154 |     if (outputManifests.isEmpty()) {
1155 |       return;
1156 |     }
1157 | 
1158 |     for (String outputManifest : outputManifests) {
1159 |       if (outputManifest.isEmpty()) {
1160 |         continue;
1161 |       }
1162 | 
1163 |       JSModuleGraph graph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
1164 |       if (shouldGenerateManifestPerModule(outputManifest)) {
1165 |         // Generate per-module manifests.
1166 |         Iterable<JSModule> modules = graph.getAllModules();
1167 |         for (JSModule module : modules) {
1168 |           Writer out = fileNameToOutputWriter(
1169 |               expandManifest(module, outputManifest));
1170 |           printManifestTo(module.getInputs(), out);
1171 |           out.close();
1172 |         }
1173 |       } else {
1174 |         // Generate a single file manifest.
1175 |         Writer out = fileNameToOutputWriter(
1176 |             expandManifest(null, outputManifest));
1177 |         if (graph == null) {
1178 |           printManifestTo(compiler.getInputsInOrder(), out);
1179 |         } else {
1180 |           printModuleGraphManifestTo(graph, out);
1181 |         }
1182 |         out.close();
1183 |       }
1184 |     }
1185 |   }

731 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
732 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
733 |       return false;
734 |     }
735 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
736 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
737 |       return false;
738 |     }
739 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
740 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
741 |         return this == that;
742 |       }
743 |       return false;
744 |     }
745 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
747 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
748 |       }
749 |       return false;
750 |     }
751 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
752 |       return false;
753 |     }
754 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
755 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
756 |   }

===== M0305 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1162-1170
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1162 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1163 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1164 |       return -1;
1165 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1166 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1167 |     } else {
1168 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1169 |     }
1170 |   }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.StatementFusion.canFuseIntoOneStatement(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/StatementFusion.java
Original range: 46-83
Documentation:

Method code:
46 |   private static boolean canFuseIntoOneStatement(Node block) {
47 |     // Fold only statement block. NOT scripts block.
48 |     if (block.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
49 |       return false;
50 |     }
51 | 
52 |     // Nothing to do here.
53 |     if (!block.hasChildren() || block.hasOneChild()) {
54 |       return false;
55 |     }
56 | 
57 |     Node last = block.getLastChild();
58 | 
59 |     for (Node c = block.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
60 |       if (!NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(c) && c != last) {
61 |         return false;
62 |       }
63 |     }
64 | 
65 |     // TODO(user): Support more control statement for fusion.
66 |     // FOR
67 |     switch(last.getType()) {
68 |       case Token.IF:
69 |       case Token.THROW:
70 |       case Token.SWITCH:
71 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
72 |         return true;
73 |       case Token.RETURN:
74 |         // We don't want to add a new return value.
75 |         return last.hasChildren();
76 |       case Token.FOR:
77 |         return NodeUtil.isForIn(last) &&
78 |             // Avoid cases where we have for(var x = foo() in a) { ....
79 |             !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(last.getFirstChild());
80 |     }
81 | 
82 |     return false;
83 |   }
