===== M0312 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.collect(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 271-377
Documentation:

Method code:
271 |     public void collect(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
272 |       if (nodeFilter != null && !nodeFilter.apply(n)) {
273 |         return;
274 |       }
275 | 
276 |       // If we are traversing the externs, then we save a pointer to the scope
277 |       // generated by them, so that we can do lookups in it later.
278 |       if (externsRoot != null && n == externsRoot) {
279 |         externsScope = t.getScope();
280 |       }
281 | 
282 |       String name;
283 |       boolean isSet = false;
284 |       Name.Type type = Name.Type.OTHER;
285 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
286 | 
287 |       switch (n.getType()) {
288 |         case Token.GET:
289 |         case Token.SET:
290 |         case Token.STRING:
291 |           // This may be a key in an object literal declaration.
292 |           name = null;
293 |           if (parent != null && parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
294 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(n);
295 |           }
296 |           if (name == null) return;
297 |           isSet = true;
298 |           switch (n.getType()) {
299 |             case Token.STRING:
300 |               type = getValueType(n.getFirstChild());
301 |               break;
302 |             case Token.GET:
303 |               type = Name.Type.GET;
304 |               break;
305 |             case Token.SET:
306 |               type = Name.Type.SET;
307 |               break;
308 |             default:
309 |               throw new IllegalStateException("unexpected:" + n);
310 |           }
311 |           break;
312 |         case Token.NAME:
313 |           // This may be a variable get or set.
314 |           if (parent != null) {
315 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
316 |               case Token.VAR:
317 |                 isSet = true;
318 |                 Node rvalue = n.getFirstChild();
319 |                 type = rvalue == null ? Name.Type.OTHER : getValueType(rvalue);
320 |                 break;
321 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
322 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
323 |                   isSet = true;
324 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
325 |                 }
326 |                 break;
327 |               case Token.GETPROP:
328 |                 return;
329 |               case Token.FUNCTION:
330 |                 Node gramps = parent.getParent();
331 |                 if (gramps == null ||
332 |                     NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) return;
333 |                 isSet = true;
334 |                 type = Name.Type.FUNCTION;
335 |                 break;
336 |             }
337 |           }
338 |           name = n.getString();
339 |           break;
340 |         case Token.GETPROP:
341 |           // This may be a namespaced name get or set.
342 |           if (parent != null) {
343 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
344 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
345 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
346 |                   isSet = true;
347 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
348 |                   isPropAssign = true;
349 |                 }
350 |                 break;
351 |               case Token.GETPROP:
352 |                 return;
353 |             }
354 |           }
355 |           name = n.getQualifiedName();
356 |           if (name == null) return;
357 |           break;
358 |         default:
359 |           return;
360 |       }
361 | 
362 |       // We are only interested in global names.
363 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
364 |       if (!isGlobalNameReference(name, scope)) {
365 |         return;
366 |       }
367 | 
368 |       if (isSet) {
369 |         if (isGlobalScope(scope)) {
370 |           handleSetFromGlobal(t, n, parent, name, isPropAssign, type);
371 |         } else {
372 |           handleSetFromLocal(t, n, parent, name);
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         handleGet(t, n, parent, name);
376 |       }
377 |     }

===== M0313 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.CollectDefines.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 283-367
Documentation:

Method code:
283 |     public  void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
284 |       RefInfo refInfo = allRefInfo.get(n);
285 |       if (refInfo != null) {
286 |         Ref ref = refInfo.ref;
287 |         Name name = refInfo.name;
288 |         String fullName = name.fullName();
289 |         switch (ref.type) {
290 |           case SET_FROM_GLOBAL:
291 |           case SET_FROM_LOCAL:
292 |             Node valParent = getValueParent(ref);
293 |             Node val = valParent.getLastChild();
294 |             if (valParent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN && name.isSimpleName() &&
295 |                 name.declaration == ref) {
296 |               // For defines, it's an error if a simple name is assigned
297 |               // before it's declared
298 |               compiler.report(
299 |                   t.makeError(val, INVALID_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, fullName));
300 |             } else if (processDefineAssignment(t, fullName, val, valParent)) {
301 |               // remove the assignment so that the variable is still declared,
302 |               // but no longer assigned to a value, e.g.,
303 |               // DEF_FOO = 5; // becomes "5;"
304 | 
305 |               // We can't remove the ASSIGN/VAR when we're still visiting its
306 |               // children, so we'll have to come back later to remove it.
307 |               refInfo.name.removeRef(ref);
308 |               lvalueToRemoveLater = valParent;
309 |             }
310 |             break;
311 |           default:
312 |             if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
313 |               // Treat this as a reference to a define in the global scope.
314 |               // After this point, the define must not be reassigned,
315 |               // or it's an error.
316 |               DefineInfo info = assignableDefines.get(fullName);
317 |               if (info != null) {
318 |                 setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
319 |                 assignableDefines.remove(fullName);
320 |               }
321 |             }
322 |             break;
323 |         }
324 |       }
325 | 
326 |       if (!t.inGlobalScope() &&
327 |           n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isDefine()) {
328 |         // warn about @define annotations in local scopes
329 |         compiler.report(
330 |             t.makeError(n, NON_GLOBAL_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, ""));
331 |       }
332 | 
333 |       if (lvalueToRemoveLater == n) {
334 |         lvalueToRemoveLater = null;
335 |         if (n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
336 |           Node last = n.getLastChild();
337 |           n.removeChild(last);
338 |           parent.replaceChild(n, last);
339 |         } else {
340 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.NAME);
341 |           n.removeChild(n.getFirstChild());
342 |         }
343 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
344 |       }
345 | 
346 |       if (n.getType() == Token.CALL) {
347 |         if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
348 |           // If there's a function call in the global scope,
349 |           // we just say it's unsafe and freeze all the defines.
350 |           //
351 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): We could be a lot smarter here. For example,
352 |           // ReplaceOverriddenVars keeps a call graph of all functions and
353 |           // which functions/variables that they reference, and tries
354 |           // to statically determine which functions are "safe" and which
355 |           // are not. But this would be overkill, expecially because
356 |           // the intended use of defines is with config_files, where
357 |           // all the defines are at the top of the bundle.
358 |           for (DefineInfo info : assignableDefines.values()) {
359 |             setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
360 |           }
361 | 
362 |           assignableDefines.clear();
363 |         }
364 |       }
365 | 
366 |       updateAssignAllowedStack(n, false);
367 |     }

/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();

250 |         }

254 |         }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getInfoForNameNode(Node)

/**
   * @param nameNode A name node
   * @return The JSDocInfo for the name node
   */
Method code:
2834 |   static JSDocInfo getInfoForNameNode(Node nameNode) {
2835 |     JSDocInfo info = null;
2836 |     Node parent = null;
2837 |     if (nameNode != null) {
2838 |       info = nameNode.getJSDocInfo();
2839 |       parent = nameNode.getParent();
2840 |     }
2841 | 
2842 |     if (info == null && parent != null &&
2843 |         ((parent.getType() == Token.VAR && parent.hasOneChild()) ||
2844 |           parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION)) {
2845 |       info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2846 |     }
2847 |     return info;
2848 |   }

===== M0333 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1625-1664
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1625 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1626 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1627 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1628 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1629 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1630 |       // {?} - right curly
1631 |       // {?=} - equals
1632 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1633 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1634 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1635 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1636 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1637 |       token = next();
1638 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1639 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1640 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1641 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1642 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1643 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1644 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1645 |       }
1646 | 
1647 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1648 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1649 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1650 |     } else {
1651 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1652 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1653 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1654 |           next();
1655 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1656 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1657 |           next();
1658 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1659 |         }
1660 |       }
1661 | 
1662 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1663 |     }
1664 |   }

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

/**

===== M0344 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

===== M0345 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RenameVars.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RenameVars.java
Original range: 324-384
Documentation:

Method code:
324 |   @Override
325 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
326 |     assignmentLog = new StringBuilder();
327 | 
328 |     // Do variable reference counting.
329 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, externs, new ProcessVars(true));
330 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, new ProcessVars(false));
331 | 
332 |     // Make sure that new names don't overlap with extern names.
333 |     reservedNames.addAll(externNames);
334 | 
335 |     // Rename vars, sorted by frequency of occurrence to minimize code size.
336 |     SortedSet<Assignment> varsByFrequency =
337 |         new TreeSet<Assignment>(FREQUENCY_COMPARATOR);
338 |     varsByFrequency.addAll(assignments.values());
339 | 
340 |     if (shouldShadow) {
341 |       new ShadowVariables(
342 |           compiler, assignments, varsByFrequency, pseudoNameMap).process(
343 |               externs, root);
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     // First try to reuse names from an earlier compilation.
347 |     if (prevUsedRenameMap != null) {
348 |       reusePreviouslyUsedVariableMap();
349 |     }
350 | 
351 |     // Assign names, sorted by descending frequency to minimize code size.
352 |     assignNames(varsByFrequency);
353 | 
354 |     boolean changed = false;
355 | 
356 |     // Rename the globals!
357 |     for (Node n : globalNameNodes) {
358 |       String newName = getNewGlobalName(n);
359 |       // Note: if newName is null, then oldName is an extern.
360 |       if (newName != null) {
361 |         n.setString(newName);
362 |         changed = true;
363 |       }
364 |     }
365 | 
366 |     // Rename the locals!
367 |     int count = 0;
368 |     for (Node n : localNameNodes) {
369 |       String newName = getNewLocalName(n);
370 |       if (newName != null) {
371 |         n.setString(newName);
372 |         changed = true;
373 |       }
374 |       count++;
375 |     }
376 | 
377 |     if (changed) {
378 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
379 |     }
380 | 
381 |     // Lastly, write the name assignments to the debug log.
382 |     compiler.addToDebugLog("JS var assignments:\n" + assignmentLog);
383 |     assignmentLog = null;
384 |   }

   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param node The JsDoc Comment node to parse.

   *     normal jsdoc, or no jsdoc (if the method parses to the wrong level).

166 |   private void tryReduceOperandsForOp(Node n) {
167 |     switch (n.getType()) {
168 |       case Token.ADD:

170 |         Node right = n.getLastChild();
171 |         if (!NodeUtil.mayBeString(left) && !NodeUtil.mayBeString(right)) {
172 |           tryConvertOperandsToNumber(n);
173 |         }

175 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
176 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
177 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
178 |         // TODO(johnlenz): convert these to integers.
179 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:

181 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
182 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
183 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
184 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
185 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
186 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
187 |         break;
188 |       case Token.BITNOT:

196 |       case Token.MUL:

===== M0360 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.Traversal.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 261-344
Documentation:

Method code:
261 |     @Override
262 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
263 |       if (isCallToScopeMethod(n)) {
264 |         if (!NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent)) {
265 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USED_IMPROPERLY);
266 |         }
267 |         if (n.getChildCount() != 2) {
268 |           // The goog.scope call should have exactly 1 parameter.  The first
269 |           // child is the "goog.scope" and the second should be the parameter.
270 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_HAS_BAD_PARAMETERS);
271 |         } else {
272 |           Node anonymousFnNode = n.getChildAtIndex(1);
273 |           if (!NodeUtil.isFunction(anonymousFnNode) ||
274 |               NodeUtil.getFunctionName(anonymousFnNode) != null ||
275 |               NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(anonymousFnNode).hasChildren()) {
276 |             report(t, anonymousFnNode, GOOG_SCOPE_HAS_BAD_PARAMETERS);
277 |           } else {
278 |             scopeCalls.add(n);
279 |           }
280 |         }
281 |       }
282 | 
283 | 
284 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() == 2) {
285 |         int type = n.getType();
286 |         if (type == Token.NAME && parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
287 |           if (n.hasChildren() && n.getFirstChild().isQualifiedName()) {
288 |             String name = n.getString();
289 |             Var aliasVar = t.getScope().getVar(name);
290 |             aliases.put(name, aliasVar);
291 |             aliasDefinitions.add(n);
292 |             String qualifiedName =
293 |                 aliasVar.getInitialValue().getQualifiedName();
294 |             transformation.addAlias(name, qualifiedName);
295 |             return;
296 |           } else {
297 |             // TODO(robbyw): Support using locals for private variables.
298 |             report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_NON_ALIAS_LOCAL, n.getString());
299 |           }
300 |         }
301 | 
302 |         if (type == Token.NAME && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
303 |             n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
304 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_ALIAS_REDEFINED, n.getString());
305 |         }
306 | 
307 |         if (type == Token.RETURN) {
308 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USES_RETURN);
309 |         } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
310 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_REFERENCES_THIS);
311 |         } else if (type == Token.THROW) {
312 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USES_THROW);
313 |         }
314 |       }
315 | 
316 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() >= 2) {
317 |         if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
318 |           String name = n.getString();
319 |           Var aliasVar = aliases.get(name);
320 | 
321 |           // Check if this name points to an alias.
322 |           if (aliasVar != null &&
323 |               t.getScope().getVar(name) == aliasVar) {
324 |             // Note, to support the transitive case, it's important we don't
325 |             // clone aliasedNode here.  For example,
326 |             // var g = goog; var d = g.dom; d.createElement('DIV');
327 |             // The node in aliasedNode (which is "g") will be replaced in the
328 |             // changes pass above with "goog".  If we cloned here, we'd end up
329 |             // with <code>g.dom.createElement('DIV')</code>.
330 |             Node aliasedNode = aliasVar.getInitialValue();
331 |             aliasUsages.add(new AliasedNode(n, aliasedNode));
332 |           }
333 |         }
334 | 
335 |         JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();

229 |         Var arguments = scope.getArgumentsVar();

===== M0379 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CommandLineRunner.processArgs(String[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 486-512
Documentation:

Method code:
486 |   private List<String> processArgs(String[] args) {
487 |     // Args4j has a different format that the old command-line parser.
488 |     // So we use some voodoo to get the args into the format that args4j
489 |     // expects.
490 |     Pattern argPattern = Pattern.compile("(--[a-zA-Z_]+)=(.*)");
491 |     Pattern quotesPattern = Pattern.compile("^['\"](.*)['\"]$");
492 |     List<String> processedArgs = Lists.newArrayList();
493 | 
494 |     for (String arg : args) {
495 |       Matcher matcher = argPattern.matcher(arg);
496 |       if (matcher.matches()) {
497 |         processedArgs.add(matcher.group(1));
498 | 
499 |         String value = matcher.group(2);
500 |         Matcher quotesMatcher = quotesPattern.matcher(value);
501 |         if (quotesMatcher.matches()) {
502 |           processedArgs.add(quotesMatcher.group(1));
503 |         } else {
504 |           processedArgs.add(value);
505 |         }
506 |       } else {
507 |         processedArgs.add(arg);
508 |       }
509 |     }
510 | 
511 |     return processedArgs;
512 |   }

===== M0382 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0394 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 496-543
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
496 |     private void extractForInitializer(
497 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
498 | 
499 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
500 |         next = c.getNext();
501 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
502 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
503 |         switch (c.getType()) {
504 |           case Token.LABEL:
505 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
506 |             break;
507 |           case Token.FOR:
508 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
509 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
510 |               if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
511 |                 // Transform:
512 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
513 |                 // to:
514 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
515 |                 Node newStatement = first;
516 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
517 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
518 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
519 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
520 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
521 |               }
522 |             } else if (c.getFirstChild().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
523 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
524 |               Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
525 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
526 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
527 | 
528 |               Node newStatement;
529 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
530 |               // but are handled differently.
531 |               if (init.getType() == Token.VAR) {
532 |                 newStatement = init;
533 |               } else {
534 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
535 |               }
536 | 
537 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
538 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
539 |             }
540 |             break;
541 |         }
542 |       }
543 |     }

===== M0398 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 115-145
Documentation:

Method code:
115 |   @Override
116 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
117 | 
118 |     if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
119 |       return; // Don't even brother. All global variables are likely escaped.
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     // Compute the forward reaching definition.
123 |     ControlFlowAnalysis cfa = new ControlFlowAnalysis(compiler, false, true);
124 |     // Process the body of the function.
125 |     Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isFunction(t.getScopeRoot()));
126 |     cfa.process(null, t.getScopeRoot().getLastChild());
127 |     cfg = cfa.getCfg();
128 |     reachingDef = new MustBeReachingVariableDef(cfg, t.getScope(), compiler);
129 |     reachingDef.analyze();
130 |     candidates = Lists.newLinkedList();
131 | 
132 |     // Using the forward reaching definition search to find all the inline
133 |     // candiates
134 |     new NodeTraversal(compiler, new GatherCandiates()).traverse(
135 |         t.getScopeRoot().getLastChild());
136 | 
137 |     // Compute the backward reaching use. The CFG can be reused.
138 |     reachingUses = new MaybeReachingVariableUse(cfg, t.getScope(), compiler);
139 |     reachingUses.analyze();
140 |     for (Candidate c : candidates) {
141 |       if (c.canInline()) {
142 |         c.inlineVariable();
143 |       }
144 |     }
145 |   }

===== M0409 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 82-112
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
83 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
84 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
85 | 
86 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
87 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
88 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
89 | 
90 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
91 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
92 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
93 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
94 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
95 |       }
96 | 
97 |       // Args: Declare function variables
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
99 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
100 |            a = a.getNext()) {
101 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
102 |         declareVar(a);
103 |       }
104 | 
105 |       // Body
106 |       scanVars(body, n);
107 |     } else {
108 |       // It's the global block
109 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
110 |       scanVars(n, null);
111 |     }
112 |   }

===== M0414 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1752-1766
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1752 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1753 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1754 |       return getString();
1755 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1756 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1757 |       if (left == null) {
1758 |         return null;
1759 |       }
1760 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1761 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1762 |       return "this";
1763 |     } else {
1764 |       return null;
1765 |     }
1766 |   }

===== M0432 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0436 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.collectDefines(Node,GlobalNamespace)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 171-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds all defines, and creates a {@link DefineInfo} data structure for
   * each one.
   * @return A map of {@link DefineInfo} structures, keyed by name.
   */
Method code:
171 |   private Map<String, DefineInfo> collectDefines(Node root,
172 |       GlobalNamespace namespace) {
173 |     // Find all the global names with a @define annotation
174 |     List<Name> allDefines = Lists.newArrayList();
175 |     for (Name name : namespace.getNameIndex().values()) {
176 |       if (name.docInfo != null && name.docInfo.isDefine()) {
177 |         // Process defines should not depend on check types being enabled,
178 |         // so we look for the JSDoc instead of the inferred type.
179 |         if (isValidDefineType(name.docInfo.getType())) {
180 |           allDefines.add(name);
181 |         } else {
182 |           JSError error = JSError.make(
183 |               name.declaration.getSourceName(),
184 |               name.declaration.node,
185 |               INVALID_DEFINE_TYPE_ERROR);
186 |           compiler.report(error);
187 |         }
188 |       } else {
189 |         for (Ref ref : name.getRefs()) {
190 |           if (ref == name.declaration) {
191 |             // Declarations were handled above.
192 |             continue;
193 |           }
194 | 
195 |           Node n = ref.node;
196 |           Node parent = ref.node.getParent();
197 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
198 |           if (info == null &&
199 |               parent.getType() == Token.VAR && parent.hasOneChild()) {
200 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
201 |           }
202 | 
203 |           if (info != null && info.isDefine()) {
204 |             allDefines.add(name);
205 |             break;
206 |           }
207 |         }
208 |       }
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     CollectDefines pass = new CollectDefines(compiler, allDefines);
212 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, pass);
213 |     return pass.getAllDefines();
214 |   }

===== M0437 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isExprAssign(Node)

Original range: 1521-1524
Documentation:
/**
   * Is this node an assignment expression statement?
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if {@code n} is EXPR_RESULT and {@code n}'s
   *     first child is ASSIGN
   */
Method code:
1521 |   static boolean isExprAssign(Node n) {

===== M0442 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 86-153
Documentation:

Method code:
86 |   private Node tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node subtree) {
87 |     Node left = subtree.getFirstChild();
88 | 
89 |     if (left == null) {
90 |       return subtree;
91 |     }
92 | 
93 |     Node right = left.getNext();
94 | 
95 |     if (right == null) {
96 |       return subtree;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // If we've reached here, node is truly a binary operator.
100 |     switch(subtree.getType()) {
101 |       case Token.GETPROP:
102 |         return tryFoldGetProp(subtree, left, right);
103 | 
104 |       case Token.GETELEM:
105 |         return tryFoldGetElem(subtree, left, right);
106 | 
107 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
108 |         return tryFoldInstanceof(subtree, left, right);
109 | 
110 |       case Token.AND:
111 |       case Token.OR:
112 |         return tryFoldAndOr(subtree, left, right);
113 | 
114 |       case Token.LSH:
115 |       case Token.RSH:
116 |       case Token.URSH:
117 |         return tryFoldShift(subtree, left, right);
118 | 
119 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
120 |         return tryFoldAssign(subtree, left, right);
121 | 
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |         return tryFoldAdd(subtree, left, right);
124 | 
125 |       case Token.SUB:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.MOD:
128 |         return tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
129 | 
130 |       case Token.MUL:
131 |       case Token.BITAND:
132 |       case Token.BITOR:
133 |       case Token.BITXOR:
134 |         Node result = tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
135 |         if (result != subtree) {
136 |           return result;
137 |         }
138 |         return tryFoldLeftChildOp(subtree, left, right);
139 | 
140 |       case Token.LT:
141 |       case Token.GT:
142 |       case Token.LE:
143 |       case Token.GE:
144 |       case Token.EQ:
145 |       case Token.NE:
146 |       case Token.SHEQ:
147 |       case Token.SHNE:
148 |         return tryFoldComparison(subtree, left, right);
149 | 
150 |       default:
151 |         return subtree;
152 |     }
153 |   }

91 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {

===== M0447 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 582-639
Documentation:

Method code:
582 |     @Override
583 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
584 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
585 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
586 |       if (name == null) {
587 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
588 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
589 |           errorReporter.error(
590 |             "unnamed function statement",
591 |             sourceName,
592 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
593 |         }
594 |         name = new Name();
595 |         name.setIdentifier("");
596 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
597 |       }
598 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
599 |       Node newName = transform(name);
600 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
601 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
602 |         // declaration.
603 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
604 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
605 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
606 |         // same line as the function keyword.
607 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
608 |             functionNode.getLp();
609 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
610 |       }
611 | 
612 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
613 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
614 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
615 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
616 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
617 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
618 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
619 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
620 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
621 |       if (fnName != null) {
622 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
623 |       } else {
624 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
625 |       }
626 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
627 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
628 | 
629 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
630 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
631 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
632 |       }
633 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
634 | 
635 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
636 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
637 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
638 |      return node;
639 |     }

===== M0449 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CoalesceVariableNames.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CoalesceVariableNames.java
Original range: 91-124
Documentation:

Method code:
91 |   @Override
92 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
93 |     // TODO(user): We CAN do this in the global scope, just need to be
94 |     // careful when something is exported. Liveness uses bit-vector for live
95 |     // sets so I don't see compilation time will be a problem for running this
96 |     // pass in the global scope.
97 |     if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
98 |       return;
99 |     }
100 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
101 |     ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg = t.getControlFlowGraph();
102 | 
103 |     LiveVariablesAnalysis liveness =
104 |         new LiveVariablesAnalysis(cfg, scope, compiler);
105 |     // If the function has exactly 2 params, mark them as escaped. This is
106 |     // a work-around for an IE bug where it throws an exception if you
107 |     // write to the parameters of the callback in a sort(). See:
108 |     // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=58
109 |     if (scope.getRootNode().getFirstChild().getNext().getChildCount() == 2) {
110 |       liveness.markAllParametersEscaped();
111 |     }
112 |     liveness.analyze();
113 | 
114 |     UndiGraph<Var, Void> interferenceGraph =
115 |         computeVariableNamesInterferenceGraph(
116 |             t, cfg, liveness.getEscapedLocals());
117 | 
118 |     GraphColoring<Var, Void> coloring =
119 |         new GreedyGraphColoring<Var, Void>(interferenceGraph,
120 |             coloringTieBreaker);
121 | 
122 |     coloring.color();
123 |     colorings.push(coloring);
124 |   }

===== M0451 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodeRemoval.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodeRemoval.java
Original range: 198-230
Documentation:

Method code:
198 |   @Override
199 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
200 |     List<Callback> passes = Lists.newArrayList();
201 |     if (removeAbstractMethods) {
202 |       passes.add(new FindAbstractMethods());
203 |     }
204 |     if (removeAssertionCalls) {
205 |       passes.add(new FindAssertionCalls());
206 |     }
207 |     CombinedCompilerPass.traverse(compiler, root, passes);
208 | 
209 |     for (RemovableAssignment assignment : abstractMethodAssignmentNodes) {
210 |       assignment.remove();
211 |     }
212 | 
213 |     for (Node call : assertionCalls) {
214 |       // If the assertion is an expression, just strip the whole thing.
215 |       Node parent = call.getParent();
216 |       if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent)) {
217 |         parent.getParent().removeChild(parent);
218 |       } else {
219 |         // Otherwise, replace the assertion with its first argument,
220 |         // which is the return value of the assertion.
221 |         Node firstArg = call.getFirstChild().getNext();
222 |         if (firstArg == null) {
223 |           parent.replaceChild(call, NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(call));
224 |         } else {
225 |           parent.replaceChild(call, firstArg.detachFromParent());
226 |         }
227 |       }
228 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
229 |     }
230 |   }

   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList

1576 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {

===== M0460 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1077-1097
Documentation:

Method code:
1077 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1078 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1079 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1080 |       return s;
1081 |     }
1082 | 
1083 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1084 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1085 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1086 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1087 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1088 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1089 |       // crazy.
1090 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1091 |         sb.append(c);
1092 |       } else {
1093 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1094 |       }
1095 |     }
1096 |     return sb.toString();
1097 |   }

186 |   private void endPass(Node externs, Node root) {
187 |     Preconditions.checkState(currentTracer != null && currentPassName != null);
188 | 
189 |     String passToCheck = currentPassName;
190 |     try {
191 |       stopTracer(currentTracer, currentPassName);
192 |       currentPassName = null;
193 |       currentTracer = null;
194 | 
195 |       maybeSanityCheck(externs, root);
196 |     } catch (Exception e) {
197 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Remove this once the normalization checks report
198 |       // errors instead of exceptions.
199 |       throw new RuntimeException("Sanity check failed for " + passToCheck, e);
200 |     }
201 |   }

===== M0475 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.doStatementNormalizations(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 434-455
Documentation:
/**
     * Do normalizations that introduce new siblings or parents.
     */
Method code:
434 |     private void doStatementNormalizations(
435 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
436 |       if (n.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
437 |         normalizeLabels(n);
438 |       }
439 | 
440 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs and LABELs, as all these
441 |       // are the only legal place for VARs and FOR statements.
442 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n) || n.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
443 |         extractForInitializer(n, null, null);
444 |       }
445 | 
446 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs, as all these
447 |       // are the only legal place for VARs.
448 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
449 |         splitVarDeclarations(n);
450 |       }
451 |

===== M0479 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 260-282
Documentation:

Method code:
260 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
261 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
262 |       return;
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     try {
266 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
267 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
268 | 
269 |       sourceName = "";
270 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
271 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
272 | 
273 |       for (Node root : roots) {
274 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
275 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       popScope();
279 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
280 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
281 |     }
282 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameGenerator.reserveCharacters(char[],char[])

Documentation:
/**
   * Provides the array of available characters based on the specified arrays.
   * @param chars The list of characters that are legal
   * @param reservedCharacters The characters that should not be used
   * @return An array of characters to use. Will return the chars array if
   *    reservedCharacters is null or empty, otherwise creates a new array.
   */
Method code:
78 |   static char[] reserveCharacters(char[] chars, char[] reservedCharacters) {
79 |     if (reservedCharacters == null || reservedCharacters.length == 0) {
80 |       return chars;
81 |     }
82 |     Set<Character> charSet = Sets.newLinkedHashSet(Chars.asList(chars));
83 |     for (char reservedCharacter : reservedCharacters) {
84 |       charSet.remove(reservedCharacter);
85 |     }
86 |     return Chars.toArray(charSet);
87 |   }

===== M0490 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 553-571
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
553 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
554 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
555 |         next = c.getNext();
556 |         if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
557 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
558 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
559 |           }
560 | 
561 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
562 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
563 |             c.removeChild(name);
564 |             Node newVar = new Node(
565 |                 Token.VAR, name, n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
566 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
567 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
568 |           }
569 |         }
570 |       }
571 |     }

===== M0492 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.isBlockBoundary(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 191-221
Documentation:
/**
   * @return true if this node marks the start of a new basic block
   */
Method code:
191 |   private static boolean isBlockBoundary(Node n, Node parent) {
192 |     if (parent != null) {
193 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
194 |         case Token.DO:
195 |         case Token.FOR:
196 |         case Token.TRY:
197 |         case Token.WHILE:
198 |         case Token.WITH:
199 |           // NOTE: TRY has up to 3 child blocks:
200 |           // TRY
201 |           //   BLOCK
202 |           //   BLOCK
203 |           //     CATCH
204 |           //   BLOCK
205 |           // Note that there is an explcit CATCH token but no explicit
206 |           // FINALLY token. For simplicity, we consider each BLOCK
207 |           // a separate basic BLOCK.
208 |           return true;
209 |         case Token.AND:
210 |         case Token.HOOK:
211 |         case Token.IF:
212 |         case Token.OR:
213 |           // The first child of a conditional is not a boundary,
214 |           // but all the rest of the children are.
215 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
216 | 
217 |       }
218 |     }
219 | 
220 |     return n.getType() == Token.CASE;
221 |   }

===== M0503 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0531 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.interpretAssigns()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 703-748
Documentation:
/**
   * Look at all the property assigns to all variables.
   * These may or may not count as references. For example,
   *
   * <code>
   * var x = {};
   * x.foo = 3; // not a reference.
   * var y = foo();
   * y.foo = 3; // is a reference.
   * </code>
   *
   * Interpreting assigments could mark a variable as referenced that
   * wasn't referenced before, in order to keep it alive. Because we find
   * references by lazily traversing subtrees, marking a variable as
   * referenced could trigger new traversals of new subtrees, which could
   * find new references.
   *
   * Therefore, this interpretation needs to be run to a fixed point.
   */
Method code:
703 |   private void interpretAssigns() {
704 |     boolean changes = false;
705 |     do {
706 |       changes = false;
707 | 
708 |       // We can't use traditional iterators and iterables for this list,
709 |       // because our lazily-evaluated continuations will modify it while
710 |       // we traverse it.
711 |       for (int current = 0; current < maybeUnreferenced.size(); current++) {
712 |         Var var = maybeUnreferenced.get(current);
713 |         if (referenced.contains(var)) {
714 |           maybeUnreferenced.remove(current);
715 |           current--;
716 |         } else {
717 |           boolean assignedToUnknownValue = false;
718 |           boolean hasPropertyAssign = false;
719 | 
720 |           if (var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.VAR &&
721 |               !NodeUtil.isForIn(var.getParentNode().getParent())) {
722 |             Node value = var.getInitialValue();
723 |             assignedToUnknownValue = value != null &&
724 |                 !NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(value, true);
725 |           } else {
726 |             // This was initialized to a function arg or a catch param
727 |             // or a for...in variable.
728 |             assignedToUnknownValue = true;
729 |           }
730 | 
731 |           for (Assign assign : assignsByVar.get(var)) {
732 |             if (assign.isPropertyAssign) {
733 |               hasPropertyAssign = true;
734 |             } else if (!NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(
735 |                 assign.assignNode.getLastChild(), true)) {
736 |               assignedToUnknownValue = true;
737 |             }
738 |           }
739 | 
740 |           if (assignedToUnknownValue && hasPropertyAssign) {
741 |             changes = markReferencedVar(var) || changes;
742 |             maybeUnreferenced.remove(current);
743 |             current--;
744 |           }
745 |         }
746 |       }
747 |     } while (changes);
748 |   }

===== M0533 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalVarReferenceMap.updateGlobalVarReferences(Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalVarReferenceMap.java
Original range: 97-115
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the internal reference map based on the provided parameters. If
   * {@code scriptRoot} is not SCRIPT, it basically replaces the internal map
   * with the new one, otherwise it replaces all the information associated to
   * the given script.
   *
   * @param refMapPatch The reference map result of a
   *     {@link ReferenceCollectingCallback} pass which might be collected from
   *     the whole AST or just a sub-tree associated to a SCRIPT node.
   * @param root AST sub-tree root on which reference collection was done.
   */
Method code:
97 |   void updateGlobalVarReferences(Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>
98 |       refMapPatch, Node root) {
99 |     if (refMap == null || root.getType() != Token.SCRIPT) {
100 |       resetGlobalVarReferences(refMapPatch);
101 |       return;
102 |     }
103 |     String sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(root);
104 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(sourceName);
105 |     // Note there are two assumptions here (i) the order of compiler inputs
106 |     // has not changed and (ii) all references are in the order they appear
107 |     // in AST (this is enforced in ReferenceCollectionCallback).
108 |     removeScriptReferences(sourceName);
109 |     for (Entry<Var, ReferenceCollection> entry : refMapPatch.entrySet()) {
110 |       Var var = entry.getKey();
111 |       if (var.isGlobal()) {
112 |         replaceReferences(var.getName(), sourceName, entry.getValue());
113 |       }
114 |     }
115 |   }

===== M0557 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.anonymous_L1396.call()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1397-1450
Documentation:

Method code:
1397 |       public Void call() throws Exception {
1398 |         if (options.printInputDelimiter) {
1399 |           if ((cb.getLength() > 0) && !cb.endsWith("\n")) {
1400 |             cb.append("\n");  // Make sure that the label starts on a new line
1401 |           }
1402 |           Preconditions.checkState(root.getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
1403 | 
1404 |           String delimiter = options.inputDelimiter;
1405 | 
1406 |           String sourceName = (String)root.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
1407 |           Preconditions.checkState(sourceName != null);
1408 |           Preconditions.checkState(!sourceName.isEmpty());
1409 | 
1410 |           delimiter = delimiter.replaceAll("%name%", sourceName)
1411 |             .replaceAll("%num%", String.valueOf(inputSeqNum));
1412 | 
1413 |           cb.append(delimiter)
1414 |             .append("\n");
1415 |         }
1416 |         if (root.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
1417 |             root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null) {
1418 |           cb.append("/*\n")
1419 |             .append(root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense())
1420 |             .append("*/\n");
1421 |         }
1422 | 
1423 |         // If there is a valid source map, then indicate to it that the current
1424 |         // root node's mappings are offset by the given string builder buffer.
1425 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null) {
1426 |           sourceMap.setStartingPosition(
1427 |               cb.getLineIndex(), cb.getColumnIndex());
1428 |         }
1429 | 
1430 |         // if LanguageMode is ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT, only print 'use strict'
1431 |         // for the first input file
1432 |         String code = toSource(root, sourceMap);
1433 |         if (!code.isEmpty()) {
1434 |           cb.append(code);
1435 | 
1436 |           // In order to avoid parse ambiguity when files are concatenated
1437 |           // together, all files should end in a semi-colon. Do a quick
1438 |           // heuristic check if there's an obvious semi-colon already there.
1439 |           int length = code.length();
1440 |           char lastChar = code.charAt(length - 1);
1441 |           char secondLastChar = length >= 2 ?
1442 |               code.charAt(length - 2) : '\0';
1443 |           boolean hasSemiColon = lastChar == ';' ||
1444 |               (lastChar == '\n' && secondLastChar == ';');
1445 |           if (!hasSemiColon) {
1446 |             cb.append(";");
1447 |           }
1448 |         }
1449 |         return null;
1450 |       }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getOrCreateName(String)

Documentation:
/**
     * Gets a {@link Name} instance for a global name. Creates it if necessary,
     * as well as instances for any of its prefixes that are not yet defined.
     *
     * @param name A global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c.d")
     * @return The {@link Name} instance for {@code name}
     */
Method code:
787 |     Name getOrCreateName(String name) {
788 |       Name node = nameMap.get(name);
789 |       if (node == null) {
790 |         int i = name.lastIndexOf('.');
791 |         if (i >= 0) {
792 |           String parentName = name.substring(0, i);
793 |           Name parent = getOrCreateName(parentName);
794 |           node = parent.addProperty(name.substring(i + 1), inExterns);
795 |         } else {
796 |           node = new Name(name, null, inExterns);
797 |           globalNames.add(node);
798 |         }
799 |         nameMap.put(name, node);
800 |       }
801 |       return node;
802 |     }

===== M0571 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 727-765
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name's longest prefix before the "prototype" property if the
     * name includes the "prototype" property. Does nothing otherwise.
     *
     * @param t The current node traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @return Whether the name was handled
     */
Method code:
727 |     boolean maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent,
728 |         String name) {
729 |       // We use a string-based approach instead of inspecting the parse tree
730 |       // to avoid complexities with object literals, possibly nested, beneath
731 |       // assignments.
732 | 
733 |       int numLevelsToRemove;
734 |       String prefix;
735 |       if (name.endsWith(".prototype")) {
736 |         numLevelsToRemove = 1;
737 |         prefix = name.substring(0, name.length() - 10);
738 |       } else {
739 |         int i = name.indexOf(".prototype.");
740 |         if (i == -1) {
741 |           return false;
742 |         }
743 |         prefix = name.substring(0, i);
744 |         numLevelsToRemove = 2;
745 |         i = name.indexOf('.', i + 11);
746 |         while (i >= 0) {
747 |           numLevelsToRemove++;
748 |           i = name.indexOf('.', i + 1);
749 |         }
750 |       }
751 | 
752 |       if (parent != null && NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
753 |         // Object literal keys have no prefix that's referenced directly per
754 |         // key, so we're done.
755 |         return true;
756 |       }
757 | 
758 |       for (int i = 0; i < numLevelsToRemove; i++) {
759 |         parent = n;
760 |         n = n.getFirstChild();
761 |       }
762 | 
763 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, prefix, Ref.Type.PROTOTYPE_GET);
764 |       return true;
765 |     }

249 |                          new RefInfo(name.declaration, name));
250 |         }
251 |         for (Ref ref : name.getRefs()) {
252 |           if (ref == name.declaration) {
253 |             // Declarations were handled above.
254 |             continue;
255 |           }
256 | 
257 |           // If there's a TWIN def, only put one of the twins in.
258 |           if (ref.getTwin() == null || !ref.getTwin().isSet()) {
259 |             allRefInfo.put(ref.node, new RefInfo(ref, name));
260 |           }
261 |         }
262 |       }
263 |     }

===== M0589 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefaultPassConfig.getMainOptimizationLoop()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefaultPassConfig.java
Original range: 637-666
Documentation:
/** Creates the passes for the main optimization loop. */
Method code:
637 |   private List<PassFactory> getMainOptimizationLoop() {
638 |     List<PassFactory> passes = Lists.newArrayList();
639 |     if (options.inlineGetters) {
640 |       passes.add(inlineSimpleMethods);
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     passes.addAll(getCodeRemovingPasses());
644 | 
645 |     if (options.inlineFunctions || options.inlineLocalFunctions) {
646 |       passes.add(inlineFunctions);
647 |     }
648 | 
649 |     boolean runOptimizeCalls = options.optimizeCalls
650 |         || options.optimizeParameters
651 |         || options.optimizeReturns;
652 | 
653 |     if (options.removeUnusedVars || options.removeUnusedLocalVars) {
654 |       if (options.deadAssignmentElimination) {
655 |         passes.add(deadAssignmentsElimination);
656 |       }
657 |       if (!runOptimizeCalls) {
658 |         passes.add(removeUnusedVars);
659 |       }
660 |     }
661 |     if (runOptimizeCalls) {
662 |       passes.add(optimizeCallsAndRemoveUnusedVars);
663 |     }
664 |     assertAllLoopablePasses(passes);
665 |     return passes;
666 |   }

===== M0596 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 820-847
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
820 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
821 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
822 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
823 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
824 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
825 |           return;
826 |         }
827 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
828 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
829 | 
830 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
831 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
832 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
835 |           }
836 |         } else { // Has a catch.
837 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
838 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
839 |             return;
840 |           } else {
841 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
842 |           }
843 |         }
844 |         lastJump = handler;
845 |       }
846 |     }
847 |   }

===== M0608 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.LoopInternal.optimizePasses()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 362-381
Documentation:
/** Re-arrange the passes in an optimal order. */
Method code:
362 |     private void optimizePasses() {
363 |       // It's important that this ordering is deterministic, so that
364 |       // multiple compiles with the same input produce exactly the same
365 |       // results.
366 |       //
367 |       // To do this, grab any passes we recognize, and move them to the end
368 |       // in an "optimal" order.
369 |       List<NamedPass> optimalPasses = Lists.newArrayList();
370 |       for (String passName : OPTIMAL_ORDER) {
371 |         for (NamedPass pass : myPasses) {
372 |           if (pass.name.equals(passName)) {
373 |             optimalPasses.add(pass);
374 |             break;
375 |           }
376 |         }
377 |       }
378 | 
379 |       myPasses.removeAll(optimalPasses);
380 |       myPasses.addAll(optimalPasses);
381 |     }

===== M0617 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DeadAssignmentsElimination.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DeadAssignmentsElimination.java
Original range: 69-99
Documentation:

Method code:
69 |   @Override
70 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
71 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
72 |     // Global scope _SHOULD_ work, however, liveness won't finish without
73 |     // -Xmx1024 in closure. We might have to look at coding conventions for
74 |     // exported variables as well.
75 |     if (scope.isGlobal()) {
76 |       return;
77 |     }
78 | 
79 |     // We are not going to do any dead assignment elimination in when there is
80 |     // at least one inner function because in most browsers, when there is a
81 |     // closure, ALL the variables are saved (escaped).
82 |     Node fnBlock = t.getScopeRoot().getLastChild();
83 |     if (NodeUtil.containsFunction(fnBlock)) {
84 |       return;
85 |     }
86 | 
87 |     // We don't do any dead assignment elimination if there are no assigns
88 |     // to eliminate. :)
89 |     if (!NodeUtil.has(fnBlock, matchRemovableAssigns,
90 |             Predicates.<Node>alwaysTrue())) {
91 |       return;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     // Computes liveness information first.
95 |     ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg = t.getControlFlowGraph();
96 |     liveness = new LiveVariablesAnalysis(cfg, scope, compiler);
97 |     liveness.analyze();
98 |     tryRemoveDeadAssignments(t, cfg);
99 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 304-336
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
304 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
305 |     if (prototype == null) {
306 |       return false;
307 |     }
308 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
309 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
310 |       return false;
311 |     }
312 | 
313 |     boolean replacedPrototype = prototype != null;
314 |     this.prototype = prototype;
315 | 
316 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
317 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
318 |       if (superClass != null) {
319 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
320 |       }
321 | 
322 |       if (isInterface()) {
323 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
324 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
325 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
326 |           }
327 |         }
328 |       }
329 |     }
330 | 
331 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
332 |       clearCachedValues();
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     return true;
336 |   }

===== M0636 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.removeUnreferencedVars()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 779-845
Documentation:
/**
   * Removes any vars in the scope that were not referenced. Removes any
   * assignments to those variables as well.
   */
Method code:
779 |   private void removeUnreferencedVars() {
780 |     CodingConvention convention = codingConvention;
781 | 
782 |     for (Iterator<Var> it = maybeUnreferenced.iterator(); it.hasNext(); ) {
783 |       Var var = it.next();
784 | 
785 |       // Remove calls to inheritance-defining functions where the unreferenced
786 |       // class is the subclass.
787 |       for (Node exprCallNode : inheritsCalls.get(var)) {
788 |         NodeUtil.removeChild(exprCallNode.getParent(), exprCallNode);
789 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
790 |       }
791 | 
792 |       // Regardless of what happens to the original declaration,
793 |       // we need to remove all assigns, because they may contain references
794 |       // to other unreferenced variables.
795 |       removeAllAssigns(var);
796 | 
797 |       compiler.addToDebugLog("Unreferenced var: " + var.name);
798 |       Node nameNode = var.nameNode;
799 |       Node toRemove = nameNode.getParent();
800 |       Node parent = toRemove.getParent();
801 | 
802 |       Preconditions.checkState(
803 |           toRemove.getType() == Token.VAR ||
804 |           toRemove.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
805 |           toRemove.getType() == Token.LP &&
806 |           parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION,
807 |           "We should only declare vars and functions and function args");
808 | 
809 |       if (toRemove.getType() == Token.LP &&
810 |           parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
811 |         // Don't remove function arguments here. That's a special case
812 |         // that's taken care of in removeUnreferencedFunctionArgs.
813 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(toRemove)) {
814 |         if (!preserveFunctionExpressionNames) {
815 |           toRemove.getFirstChild().setString("");
816 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
817 |         }
818 |         // Don't remove bleeding functions.
819 |       } else if (parent != null &&
820 |           parent.getType() == Token.FOR &&
821 |           parent.getChildCount() < 4) {
822 |         // foreach iterations have 3 children. Leave them alone.
823 |       } else if (toRemove.getType() == Token.VAR &&
824 |           nameNode.hasChildren() &&
825 |           NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
826 |         // If this is a single var declaration, we can at least remove the
827 |         // declaration itself and just leave the value, e.g.,
828 |         // var a = foo(); => foo();
829 |         if (toRemove.getChildCount() == 1) {
830 |           parent.replaceChild(toRemove,
831 |               new Node(Token.EXPR_RESULT, nameNode.removeFirstChild()));
832 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
833 |         }
834 |       } else if (toRemove.getType() == Token.VAR &&
835 |           toRemove.getChildCount() > 1) {
836 |         // For var declarations with multiple names (i.e. var a, b, c),
837 |         // only remove the unreferenced name
838 |         toRemove.removeChild(nameNode);
839 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
840 |       } else if (parent != null) {
841 |         NodeUtil.removeChild(parent, toRemove);
842 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
843 |       }
844 |     }
845 |   }

===== M0647 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 380-393
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
380 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
381 |       Node ret;
382 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
383 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
384 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
385 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
386 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
387 |       } else {
388 |         ret = transform(n);
389 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
390 |       }
391 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.getType() == Token.STRING);
392 |       return ret;
393 |     }

===== M0651 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getValueType(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 456-478
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the type of a value or simple expression.
     *
     * @param n An rvalue in an assignment or variable declaration (not null)
     * @return A {@link Name.Type}
     */
Method code:
456 |     Name.Type getValueType(Node n) {
457 |       switch (n.getType()) {
458 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
459 |           return Name.Type.OBJECTLIT;
460 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
461 |           return Name.Type.FUNCTION;
462 |         case Token.OR:
463 |           // Recurse on the second value. If the first value were an object
464 |           // literal or function, then the OR would be meaningless and the
465 |           // second value would be dead code. Assume that if the second value
466 |           // is an object literal or function, then the first value will also
467 |           // evaluate to one when it doesn't evaluate to false.
468 |           return getValueType(n.getLastChild());
469 |         case Token.HOOK:
470 |           // The same line of reasoning used for the OR case applies here.
471 |           Node second = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
472 |           Name.Type t = getValueType(second);
473 |           if (t != Name.Type.OTHER) return t;
474 |           Node third = second.getNext();
475 |           return getValueType(third);
476 |       }
477 |       return Name.Type.OTHER;
478 |     }
