785 |                 case TYPEDEF:
786 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
787 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
788 | 
789 |                   Node typeNode = null;
790 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
791 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
792 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
793 |                     // it as the unknown type.
794 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
795 |                   } else {
796 |                     skipEOLs();
797 |                     token = next();
798 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
799 |                   }
800 | 
801 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
802 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
803 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
804 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
805 |                     }
806 |                   }
807 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
808 | 
809 |                   if (type == null) {
810 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
811 |                     // recovering parsing
812 |                   } else {
813 |                     switch (annotation) {
814 |                       case DEFINE:
815 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
816 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
817 |                               lineno, charno);
818 |                         }
819 |                         break;
820 | 
821 |                       case RETURN:
822 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
823 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
824 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
825 |                           break;
826 |                         }
827 | 
828 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
829 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
830 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
831 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
832 | 
833 |                           String returnDescription =
834 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
835 | 
836 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
837 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
838 |                                 returnDescription);
839 |                           }
840 | 
841 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
842 |                         } else {
843 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
844 |                         }
845 |                         continue retry;
846 | 
847 |                       case THIS:
848 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
849 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
850 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
851 |                         }
852 |                         break;
853 | 
854 |                       case TYPE:
855 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
856 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
857 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
858 |                         }
859 |                         break;
860 | 
861 |                       case TYPEDEF:
862 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
863 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
864 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
865 |                         }
866 |                         break;
867 |                     }
868 |                   }
869 | 
870 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
871 |                   continue retry;
872 |               }
873 |             }
874 |           }
875 |           break;
876 | 
877 |         case EOC:
878 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
879 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
880 |           }
881 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
882 |           return true;
883 | 
884 |         case EOF:
885 |           // discard any accumulated information
886 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
887 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
888 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
889 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
890 |           return false;
891 | 
892 |         case EOL:
893 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
894 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
895 |           }
896 |           token = next();
897 |           continue retry;
898 | 
899 |         default:
900 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
901 |             token = next();
902 |             continue retry;
903 |           } else {
904 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
905 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
906 |             continue retry;
907 |           }
908 |       }
909 | 
910 |       // next token
911 |       token = next();
912 |     }
913 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 64-96
Documentation:

Method code:
64 |   @Override
65 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
66 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
67 |     Renamer renamer;
68 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
69 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used the starting context can not
70 |       // be a function.
71 |       Preconditions.checkState(
72 |           declarationRoot.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
73 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
74 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
75 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
76 |     } else {
77 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     if (declarationRoot.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
81 |       // Add the function parameters
82 |       Node fnParams = declarationRoot.getFirstChild().getNext();
83 |       for (Node c = fnParams.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
84 |         String name = c.getString();
85 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
86 |       }
87 | 
88 |       // Add the function body declarations
89 |       Node functionBody = declarationRoot.getLastChild();
90 |       findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
91 |     } else {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }

===== M0665 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.hotSwapScript(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 97-136
Documentation:

Method code:
97 |   @Override
98 |   public void hotSwapScript(Node root) {
99 |     Traversal traversal = new Traversal();
100 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, traversal);
101 | 
102 |     if (!traversal.hasErrors()) {
103 | 
104 |       // Apply the aliases.
105 |       for (AliasUsage aliasUsage : traversal.getAliasUsages()) {
106 |         aliasUsage.applyAlias();
107 |       }
108 | 
109 |       // Remove the alias definitions.
110 |       for (Node aliasDefinition : traversal.getAliasDefinitions()) {
111 |         if (aliasDefinition.getParent().getType() == Token.VAR &&
112 |             aliasDefinition.getParent().hasOneChild()) {
113 |           aliasDefinition.getParent().detachFromParent();
114 |         } else {
115 |           aliasDefinition.detachFromParent();
116 |         }
117 |       }
118 | 
119 |       // Collapse the scopes.
120 |       for (Node scopeCall : traversal.getScopeCalls()) {
121 |         Node expressionWithScopeCall = scopeCall.getParent();
122 |         Node scopeClosureBlock = scopeCall.getLastChild().getLastChild();
123 |         scopeClosureBlock.detachFromParent();
124 |         expressionWithScopeCall.getParent().replaceChild(
125 |             expressionWithScopeCall,
126 |             scopeClosureBlock);
127 |         NodeUtil.tryMergeBlock(scopeClosureBlock);
128 |       }
129 | 
130 |       if (traversal.getAliasUsages().size() > 0 ||
131 |           traversal.getAliasDefinitions().size() > 0 ||
132 |           traversal.getScopeCalls().size() > 0) {
133 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
134 |       }
135 |     }
136 |   }

/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1100 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1101 |     switch (type) {
1102 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1103 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1104 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1105 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1106 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1107 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1108 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1109 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1110 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1111 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1112 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1113 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1114 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1115 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1116 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1117 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1118 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1119 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1120 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1121 |       case Token.EQ:
1122 |       case Token.NE:
1123 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1124 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1125 |       case Token.LT:
1126 |       case Token.GT:
1127 |       case Token.LE:
1128 |       case Token.GE:
1129 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1130 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1131 |       case Token.LSH:
1132 |       case Token.RSH:
1133 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1134 |       case Token.SUB:
1135 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1136 |       case Token.MUL:
1137 |       case Token.MOD:
1138 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1139 |       case Token.INC:
1140 |       case Token.DEC:
1141 |       case Token.NEW:
1142 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1143 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1144 |       case Token.VOID:
1145 |       case Token.NOT:
1146 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1147 |       case Token.POS:
1148 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1149 | 
1150 |       case Token.CALL:
1151 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1152 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1153 |       // Data values
1154 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1155 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1156 |       case Token.FALSE:
1157 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1158 |       case Token.NAME:
1159 |       case Token.NULL:
1160 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1161 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1162 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1163 |       case Token.STRING:
1164 |       case Token.THIS:
1165 |       case Token.TRUE:
1166 |         return 15;
1167 | 
1168 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1169 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
1170 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1171 |     }
1172 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 90-132
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

/**
   * Query the flag for the output charset.
   *
   * Let the outputCharset be the same as the input charset... except if
   * we're reading in UTF-8 by default.  By tradition, we've always
   * output ASCII to avoid various hiccups with different browsers,
   * proxies and firewalls.
   *
   * @return Name of the charset to use when writing outputs. Guaranteed to
   *    be a supported charset.
   * @throws FlagUsageException if flag is not a valid Charset name.

821 |             " is not a valid charset name.");
822 |       }
823 |       return config.charset;
824 |     }
825 |     return "US-ASCII";
826 |   }

===== M0686 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.UnreachableCodeElimination.tryRemoveUnconditionalBranching(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/UnreachableCodeElimination.java
Original range: 130-179
Documentation:
/**
   * Tries to remove n if an unconditional branch node (break, continue or
   * return) if the target of n is the same as the the follow of n. That is, if
   * we remove n, the control flow remains the same. Also if n targets to
   * another unconditional branch, this function will recursively try to remove
   * the target branch as well. The reason why we want to cascade this removal
   * is because we only run this pass once. If we have code such as
   *
   * break -> break -> break
   *
   * where all 3 break's are useless. The order of removal matters. When we
   * first look at the first break, we see that it branches to the 2nd break.
   * However, if we remove the last break, the 2nd break becomes useless and
   * finally the first break becomes useless as well.
   *
   * @return The target of this jump. If the target is also useless jump,
   *     the target of that useless jump recursively.
   */
Method code:
130 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
131 |   private Node tryRemoveUnconditionalBranching(Node n) {
132 |     /*
133 |      * For each of the unconditional branching control flow node, check to see
134 |      * if the ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode of that node is same as
135 |      * the branching target. If it is, the branch node is safe to be removed.
136 |      *
137 |      * This is not as clever as MinimizeExitPoints because it doesn't do any
138 |      * if-else conversion but it handles more complicated switch statements
139 |      * much nicer.
140 |      */
141 | 
142 |     // If n is null the target is the end of the function, nothing to do.
143 |     if (n == null) {
144 |        return n;
145 |     }
146 | 
147 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> gNode = curCfg.getDirectedGraphNode(n);
148 | 
149 |     if (gNode == null) {
150 |       return n;
151 |     }
152 | 
153 |     switch (n.getType()) {
154 |       case Token.RETURN:
155 |         if (n.hasChildren()) {
156 |           break;
157 |         }
158 |       case Token.BREAK:
159 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
160 | 
161 |         // We are looking for a control flow changing statement that always
162 |         // branches to the same node. If removing it the control flow still
163 |         // branches to that same node. It is safe to remove it.
164 |         List<DiGraphEdge<Node,Branch>> outEdges = gNode.getOutEdges();
165 |         if (outEdges.size() == 1 &&
166 |             // If there is a next node, there is no chance this jump is useless.
167 |             (n.getNext() == null || n.getNext().getType() == Token.FUNCTION)) {
168 | 
169 |           Preconditions.checkState(outEdges.get(0).getValue() == Branch.UNCOND);
170 |           Node fallThrough = computeFollowing(n);
171 |           Node nextCfgNode = outEdges.get(0).getDestination().getValue();
172 |           if (nextCfgNode == fallThrough) {
173 |             removeDeadExprStatementSafely(n);
174 |             return fallThrough;
175 |           }
176 |         }
177 |     }
178 |     return n;
179 |   }

===== M0687 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 406-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1670-1696
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1670 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1671 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1672 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1673 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1674 |       skipEOLs();
1675 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1676 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1677 |       skipEOLs();
1678 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1679 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1680 |       skipEOLs();
1681 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1682 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1683 |       String string = stream.getString();
1684 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1685 |         skipEOLs();
1686 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1687 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1688 |         return newStringNode(string);
1689 |       } else {
1690 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1691 |       }
1692 |     }
1693 | 
1694 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1695 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1696 |   }

===== M0703 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CompilerOptions.getReplacementsHelper(Map<String, Object>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CompilerOptions.java
Original range: 847-866
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a map of String->Node from a map of String->Number/String/Boolean.
   */
Method code:
847 |   private static Map<String, Node> getReplacementsHelper(
848 |       Map<String, Object> source) {
849 |     Map<String, Node> map = Maps.newHashMap();
850 |     for (Map.Entry<String, Object> entry : source.entrySet()) {
851 |       String name = entry.getKey();
852 |       Object value = entry.getValue();
853 |       if (value instanceof Boolean) {
854 |         map.put(name, ((Boolean) value).booleanValue() ?
855 |             new Node(Token.TRUE) : new Node(Token.FALSE));
856 |       } else if (value instanceof Integer) {
857 |         map.put(name, Node.newNumber(((Integer) value).intValue()));
858 |       } else if (value instanceof Double) {
859 |         map.put(name, Node.newNumber(((Double) value).doubleValue()));
860 |       } else {
861 |         Preconditions.checkState(value instanceof String);
862 |         map.put(name, Node.newString((String) value));
863 |       }
864 |     }
865 |     return map;
866 |   }

===== M0711 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 577-609
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
577 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
578 |       Preconditions.checkState(
579 |           functionBody.getParent().getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
580 |       Node previous = null;
581 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
582 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
583 |       // are already in the right place.
584 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
585 |         previous = current;
586 |         current = current.getNext();
587 |       }
588 | 
589 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
590 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
591 |       while (current != null) {
592 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
593 |         Node next = current.getNext();
594 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
595 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
596 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
597 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
598 | 
599 |           // Readd the function at the top of the function body (after any
600 |           // previous declarations).
601 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
602 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
603 |         } else {
604 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
605 |           previous = current;
606 |         }
607 |         current = next;
608 |       }
609 |     }

/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.

Method code:
350 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
351 |       Preconditions.checkState(
352 |           n.getType() == Token.NAME
353 |           || n.getType() == Token.STRING
354 |           || n.getType() == Token.GET
355 |           || n.getType() == Token.SET);

366 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&

/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
557 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
558 |       return scope;
559 |     }
560 | 
561 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
562 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
563 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
564 |       scopes.push(scope);
565 |     }
566 |     scopeRoots.clear();
567 | 
568 |     return scope;
569 |   }

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

===== M0735 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 441-456
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
441 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
442 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
443 |       Set<String> directives = null;
444 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
445 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
446 |         if (directives == null) {
447 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
448 |         } else {
449 |           directives.add(directive);
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 | 
453 |       if (directives != null) {
454 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
455 |       }
456 |     }

===== M0738 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 431-483
Documentation:

Method code:
431 |   @Override
432 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
433 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
434 |       return true;
435 |     }
436 | 
437 |     // Union types
438 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
439 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
440 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
441 |       return false;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     // record types
445 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
446 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Interfaces
450 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
451 |     // to the interface instance.
452 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
453 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
454 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
455 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
456 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
457 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
458 |           return true;
459 |         }
460 |       }
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
464 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
465 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
466 |           return true;
467 |         }
468 |       }
469 |     }
470 | 
471 |     // other prototype based objects
472 |     if (that != null) {
473 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
474 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
475 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
476 |         // to avoid guessing.
477 |         return true;
478 |       }
479 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     return false;
483 |   }

===== M0743 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.ProvidedName.replace()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 875-938
Documentation:
/**
     * Replace the provide statement.
     *
     * If we're providing a name with no definition, then create one.
     * If we're providing a name with a duplicate definition, then make sure
     * that definition becomes a declaration.
     */
Method code:
875 |     void replace() {
876 |       if (firstNode == null) {
877 |         // Don't touch the base case ('goog').
878 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
879 |         return;
880 |       }
881 | 
882 |       // Handle the case where there is a duplicate definition for an explicitly
883 |       // provided symbol.
884 |       if (candidateDefinition != null && explicitNode != null) {
885 |         explicitNode.detachFromParent();
886 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
887 | 
888 |         // Does this need a VAR keyword?
889 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
890 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(candidateDefinition) &&
891 |             !candidateDefinition.getFirstChild().isQualifiedName()) {
892 |           candidateDefinition.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_NAMESPACE, true);
893 |           Node assignNode = candidateDefinition.getFirstChild();
894 |           Node nameNode = assignNode.getFirstChild();
895 |           if (nameNode.getType() == Token.NAME) {
896 |             // Need to convert this assign to a var declaration.
897 |             Node valueNode = nameNode.getNext();
898 |             assignNode.removeChild(nameNode);
899 |             assignNode.removeChild(valueNode);
900 |             nameNode.addChildToFront(valueNode);
901 |             Node varNode = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode);
902 |             varNode.copyInformationFrom(candidateDefinition);
903 |             candidateDefinition.getParent().replaceChild(
904 |                 candidateDefinition, varNode);
905 |             nameNode.setJSDocInfo(assignNode.getJSDocInfo());
906 |             compiler.reportCodeChange();
907 |             replacementNode = varNode;
908 |           }
909 |         }
910 |       } else {
911 |         // Handle the case where there's not a duplicate definition.
912 |         replacementNode = createDeclarationNode();
913 |         if (firstModule == minimumModule) {
914 |           firstNode.getParent().addChildBefore(replacementNode, firstNode);
915 |         } else {
916 |           // In this case, the name was implicitly provided by two independent
917 |           // modules. We need to move this code up to a common module.
918 |           int indexOfDot = namespace.lastIndexOf('.');
919 |           if (indexOfDot == -1) {
920 |             // Any old place is fine.
921 |             compiler.getNodeForCodeInsertion(minimumModule)
922 |                 .addChildToBack(replacementNode);
923 |           } else {
924 |             // Add it after the parent namespace.
925 |             ProvidedName parentName =
926 |                 providedNames.get(namespace.substring(0, indexOfDot));
927 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName);
928 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName.replacementNode);
929 |             parentName.replacementNode.getParent().addChildAfter(
930 |                 replacementNode, parentName.replacementNode);
931 |           }
932 |         }
933 |         if (explicitNode != null) {
934 |           explicitNode.detachFromParent();
935 |         }
936 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
937 |       }
938 |     }

===== M0747 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckUnreachableCode.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckUnreachableCode.java
Original range: 52-75
Documentation:

Method code:
52 |   @Override
53 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
54 |     GraphNode<Node, Branch> gNode = t.getControlFlowGraph().getNode(n);
55 |     if (gNode != null && gNode.getAnnotation() != GraphReachability.REACHABLE) {
56 | 
57 |       // Only report error when there are some line number informations.
58 |       // There are synthetic nodes with no line number informations, nodes
59 |       // introduce by other passes (although not likely since this pass should
60 |       // be executed early) or some rhino bug.
61 |       if (n.getLineno() != -1 &&
62 |           // Allow spurious semi-colons and spurious breaks.
63 |           n.getType() != Token.EMPTY && n.getType() != Token.BREAK) {
64 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n, level, UNREACHABLE_CODE));
65 |         // From now on, we are going to assume the user fixed the error and not
66 |         // give more warning related to code section reachable from this node.
67 |         new GraphReachability<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>(
68 |             t.getControlFlowGraph()).recompute(n);
69 | 
70 |         // Saves time by not traversing children.
71 |         return false;
72 |       }
73 |     }
74 |     return true;
75 |   }

===== M0760 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 525-553
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a set operation is a constructor or enumeration
     * declaration. The set operation may either be an assignment to a name,
     * a variable declaration, or an object literal key mapping.
     *
     * @param n The node that represents the name being set
     * @param parent Parent node of {@code n} (an ASSIGN, VAR, or OBJLIT node)
     * @return Whether the set operation is either a constructor or enum
     *     declaration
     */
Method code:
525 |     private boolean isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node n, Node parent) {
526 |       JSDocInfo info;
527 |       int valueNodeType;
528 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
529 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
530 |           info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
531 |           valueNodeType = n.getNext().getType();
532 |           break;
533 |         case Token.VAR:
534 |           info = n.getJSDocInfo();
535 |           if (info == null) {
536 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
537 |           }
538 |           Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
539 |           valueNodeType = valueNode != null ? valueNode.getType() : Token.VOID;
540 |           break;
541 |         default:
542 |           if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent)) {
543 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
544 |             valueNodeType = Token.FUNCTION;
545 |             break;
546 |           }
547 |           return false;
548 |       }
549 |       // Heed the annotations only if they're sensibly used.
550 |       return info != null &&
551 |              (info.isConstructor() && valueNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
552 |               info.hasEnumParameterType() && valueNodeType == Token.OBJECTLIT);
553 |     }

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.CATCH:
127 |         {
128 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
129 | 
130 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
131 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
132 | 
133 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
134 |         }
135 |         break;
136 |     }
137 | 
138 |     return true;
139 |   }

===== M0771 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.optimizeSubtree(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.java
Original range: 39-74
Documentation:
// could be changed to use code from CheckUnreachableCode to do this.
Method code:
39 |   @Override
40 |   Node optimizeSubtree(Node subtree) {
41 |     switch(subtree.getType()) {
42 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
43 |         return tryFoldAssignment(subtree);
44 |       case Token.COMMA:
45 |         return tryFoldComma(subtree);
46 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
47 |       case Token.BLOCK:
48 |         return tryOptimizeBlock(subtree);
49 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
50 |         subtree = tryFoldExpr(subtree);
51 |         return subtree;
52 |       case Token.HOOK:
53 |         return tryFoldHook(subtree);
54 |       case Token.SWITCH:
55 |         return tryOptimizeSwitch(subtree);
56 |       case Token.IF:
57 |         return tryFoldIf(subtree);
58 |       case Token.WHILE:
59 |         return tryFoldWhile(subtree);
60 |        case Token.FOR: {
61 |           Node condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(subtree);
62 |           if (condition != null) {
63 |             tryFoldForCondition(condition);
64 |           }
65 |         }
66 |         return tryFoldFor(subtree);
67 |       case Token.DO:
68 |         return tryFoldDo(subtree);
69 |       case Token.TRY:
70 |         return tryFoldTry(subtree);
71 |       default:
72 |           return subtree;
73 |     }
74 |   }

44 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
45 |     switch (n.getType()) {
46 |       case Token.GET:
47 |       case Token.SET:
48 |       case Token.STRING:
49 |         if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent) &&
50 |             NodeUtil.isValidPropertyName(n.getString())) {
51 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, false);
52 |         }
53 |         break;

56 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
57 |         Node right = left.getNext();

59 |             NodeUtil.isValidPropertyName(right.getString())) {
60 |           n.removeChild(left);

64 |         }
65 |         break;

===== M0798 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.VarCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/VarCheck.java
Original range: 126-213
Documentation:

Method code:
126 |   @Override
127 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
128 |     if (n.getType() != Token.NAME) {
129 |       return;
130 |     }
131 | 
132 |     String varName = n.getString();
133 | 
134 |     // Only a function can have an empty name.
135 |     if (varName.isEmpty()) {
136 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isFunction(parent));
137 | 
138 |       // A function declaration with an empty name passes Rhino,
139 |       // but is supposed to be a syntax error according to the spec.
140 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
141 |         t.report(n, INVALID_FUNCTION_DECL);
142 |       }
143 |       return;
144 |     }
145 | 
146 |     // Check if this is a declaration for a var that has been declared
147 |     // elsewhere. If so, mark it as a duplicate.
148 |     if ((parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
149 |          NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent)) &&
150 |         varsToDeclareInExterns.contains(varName)) {
151 |       createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
152 | 
153 |       n.addSuppression("duplicate");
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     // Check that the var has been declared.
157 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
158 |     Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(varName);
159 |     if (var == null) {
160 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
161 |         // e.g. [ function foo() {} ], it's okay if "foo" isn't defined in the
162 |         // current scope.
163 |       } else {
164 |         // The extern checks are stricter, don't report a second error.
165 |         if (!strictExternCheck || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
166 |           t.report(n, UNDEFINED_VAR_ERROR, varName);
167 |         }
168 | 
169 |         if (sanityCheck) {
170 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected variable " + varName);
171 |         } else {
172 |           createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
173 |           scope.getGlobalScope().declare(varName, n,
174 |               null, getSynthesizedExternsInput());
175 |         }
176 |       }
177 |       return;
178 |     }
179 | 
180 |     CompilerInput currInput = t.getInput();
181 |     CompilerInput varInput = var.input;
182 |     if (currInput == varInput || currInput == null || varInput == null) {
183 |       // The variable was defined in the same file. This is fine.
184 |       return;
185 |     }
186 | 
187 |     // Check module dependencies.
188 |     JSModule currModule = currInput.getModule();
189 |     JSModule varModule = varInput.getModule();
190 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
191 |     if (varModule != currModule && varModule != null && currModule != null) {
192 |       if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(currModule, varModule)) {
193 |         // The module dependency was properly declared.
194 |       } else {
195 |         if (!sanityCheck && scope.isGlobal()) {
196 |           if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(varModule, currModule)) {
197 |             // The variable reference violates a declared module dependency.
198 |             t.report(n, VIOLATED_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
199 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
200 |           } else {
201 |             // The variable reference is between two modules that have no
202 |             // dependency relationship. This should probably be considered an
203 |             // error, but just issue a warning for now.
204 |             t.report(n, MISSING_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
205 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
206 |           }
207 |         } else {
208 |           t.report(n, STRICT_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
209 |                    currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
210 |         }
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 |   }

781 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {

===== M0802 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.runCallable(Callable<T>,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 572-623
Documentation:

Method code:
572 |   @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
573 |   static <T> T runCallable(
574 |       final Callable<T> callable, boolean useLargeStackThread, boolean trace) {
575 | 
576 |     // Under JRE 1.6, the jscompiler overflows the stack when running on some
577 |     // large or complex js code. Here we start a new thread with a larger
578 |     // stack in order to let the compiler do its thing, without having to
579 |     // increase the stack size for *every* thread (which is what -Xss does).
580 |     // Might want to add thread pool support for clients that compile a lot.
581 | 
582 |     final boolean dumpTraceReport = trace;
583 |     final Object[] result = new Object[1];
584 |     final Throwable[] exception = new Throwable[1];
585 |     Runnable runnable = new Runnable() {
586 |       public void run() {
587 |         try {
588 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
589 |             Tracer.initCurrentThreadTrace();
590 |           }
591 |           result[0] = callable.call();
592 |         } catch (Throwable e) {
593 |           exception[0] = e;
594 |         } finally {
595 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
596 |             Tracer.logAndClearCurrentThreadTrace();
597 |           }
598 |         }
599 |       }
600 |     };
601 | 
602 |     if (useLargeStackThread) {
603 |       Thread th = new Thread(null, runnable, "jscompiler", COMPILER_STACK_SIZE);
604 |       th.start();
605 |       while (true) {
606 |         try {
607 |           th.join();
608 |           break;
609 |         } catch (InterruptedException ignore) {
610 |           // ignore
611 |         }
612 |       }
613 |     } else {
614 |       runnable.run();
615 |     }
616 | 
617 |     // Pass on any exception caught by the runnable object.
618 |     if (exception[0] != null) {
619 |       throw new RuntimeException(exception[0]);
620 |     }
621 | 
622 |     return (T) result[0];
623 |   }

===== M0807 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/VarCheck.java
Original range: 219-236
Documentation:
/**
   * Create a new variable in a synthetic script. This will prevent
   * subsequent compiler passes from crashing.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private void createSynthesizedExternVar(String varName) {
220 |     Node nameNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, varName);
221 | 
222 |     // Mark the variable as constant if it matches the coding convention
223 |     // for constant vars.
224 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): honestly, i'm not sure how much this matters.
225 |     // AFAIK, all people who use the CONST coding convention also
226 |     // compile with undeclaredVars as errors. We have some test
227 |     // cases for this configuration though, and it makes them happier.
228 |     if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isConstant(varName)) {
229 |       nameNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
230 |     }
231 | 
232 |     getSynthesizedExternsRoot().addChildToBack(
233 |         new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode));
234 |     varsToDeclareInExterns.remove(varName);
235 |     compiler.reportCodeChange();
236 |   }

===== M0838 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Denormalize.maybeCollapseIntoForStatements(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Denormalize.java
Original range: 65-127
Documentation:
/**
   * Collapse VARs and EXPR_RESULT node into FOR loop initializers where
   * possible.
   */
Method code:
65 |   private void maybeCollapseIntoForStatements(Node n, Node parent) {
66 |     // Only SCRIPT, BLOCK, and LABELs can have FORs that can be collapsed into.
67 |     // LABELs are not supported here.
68 |     if (parent == null || !NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent)) {
69 |       return;
70 |     }
71 | 
72 |     // Is the current node something that can be in a for loop initializer?
73 |     if (!NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(n) && !NodeUtil.isVar(n)) {
74 |       return;
75 |     }
76 | 
77 |     // Is the next statement a valid FOR?
78 |     Node nextSibling = n.getNext();
79 |     if (nextSibling == null) {
80 |       return;
81 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isForIn(nextSibling)) {
82 |       Node forNode = nextSibling;
83 |       Node forVar = forNode.getFirstChild();
84 |       if (NodeUtil.isName(forVar)
85 |           && NodeUtil.isVar(n) && n.hasOneChild()) {
86 |         Node name = n.getFirstChild();
87 |         if (!name.hasChildren()
88 |             && forVar.getString().equals(name.getString())) {
89 |           // Ok, the names match, and the var declaration does not have an
90 |           // initializer. Move it into the loop.
91 |           parent.removeChild(n);
92 |           forNode.replaceChild(forVar, n);
93 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
94 |         }
95 |       }
96 |     } else if (nextSibling.getType() == Token.FOR
97 |         && nextSibling.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
98 | 
99 |       // Does the current node contain an in operator?  If so, embedding
100 |       // the expression in a for loop can cause some Javascript parsers (such
101 |       // as the Playstation 3's browser based on Access's NetFront
102 |       // browser) to fail to parse the code.
103 |       // See bug 1778863 for details.
104 |       if (NodeUtil.containsType(n, Token.IN)) {
105 |         return;
106 |       }
107 | 
108 |       // Move the current node into the FOR loop initializer.
109 |       Node forNode = nextSibling;
110 |       Node oldInitializer = forNode.getFirstChild();
111 |       parent.removeChild(n);
112 | 
113 |       Node newInitializer;
114 |       if (NodeUtil.isVar(n)) {
115 |         newInitializer = n;
116 |       } else {
117 |         // Extract the expression from EXPR_RESULT node.
118 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
119 |         newInitializer = n.getFirstChild();
120 |         n.removeChild(newInitializer);
121 |       }
122 | 
123 |       forNode.replaceChild(oldInitializer, newInitializer);
124 | 
125 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
126 |     }
127 |   }

===== M0848 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2274-2290
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2274 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2275 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2276 |     int c;
2277 |     while (true) {
2278 |       c = stream.getChar();
2279 |       if (c == ' ') {
2280 |         continue;
2281 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2282 |         matchedLc = true;
2283 |         break;
2284 |       } else {
2285 |         break;
2286 |       }
2287 |     }
2288 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2289 |     return matchedLc;
2290 |   }

===== M0857 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1924-1926
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1924 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1925 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1926 |   }

===== M0863 =====

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1158 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1159 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);

1161 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {

1164 |       } else {

1166 |       }
1167 |     }

===== M0864 =====

Method code:
296 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
297 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
298 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
299 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
300 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
301 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
302 |     } else {

304 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
305 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
306 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
307 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
308 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);

310 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);

243 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
244 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
245 |         switch (parent.getType()) {
246 |           case Token.VAR:
247 |           case Token.FUNCTION:
248 |           case Token.LP:
249 |             // These are okay.

258 |               }

===== M0897 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RenameVars.assignNames(Set<Assignment>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RenameVars.java
Original range: 441-511
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines which new names to substitute for the original names.
   */
Method code:
441 |   private void assignNames(Set<Assignment> varsToRename) {
442 |     NameGenerator globalNameGenerator =
443 |         new NameGenerator(reservedNames, prefix, reservedCharacters);
444 | 
445 |     // Local variables never need a prefix.
446 |     NameGenerator localNameGenerator =
447 |         prefix.isEmpty() ? globalNameGenerator : new NameGenerator(
448 |             reservedNames, "", reservedCharacters);
449 | 
450 |     // Generated names and the assignments for non-local vars.
451 |     List<Assignment> pendingAssignments = new ArrayList<Assignment>();
452 |     List<String> generatedNamesForAssignments = new ArrayList<String>();
453 | 
454 |     for (Assignment a : varsToRename) {
455 |       if (a.newName != null) {
456 |         continue;
457 |       }
458 | 
459 |       if (externNames.contains(a.oldName)) {
460 |         continue;
461 |       }
462 | 
463 |       String newName;
464 |       if (a.oldName.startsWith(LOCAL_VAR_PREFIX)) {
465 |         // For local variable, we make the assignment right away.
466 |         newName = localNameGenerator.generateNextName();
467 |         finalizeNameAssignment(a, newName);
468 |       } else {
469 |         // For non-local variable, delay finalizing the name assignment
470 |         // until we know how many new names we'll have of length 2, 3, etc.
471 |         newName = globalNameGenerator.generateNextName();
472 |         pendingAssignments.add(a);
473 |         generatedNamesForAssignments.add(newName);
474 |       }
475 |       reservedNames.add(newName);
476 |     }
477 | 
478 |     // Now that we have a list of generated names, and a list of variable
479 |     // Assignment objects, we assign the generated names to the vars as
480 |     // follows:
481 |     // 1) The most frequent vars get the shorter names.
482 |     // 2) If N number of vars are going to be assigned names of the same
483 |     //    length, we assign the N names based on the order at which the vars
484 |     //    first appear in the source. This makes the output somewhat less
485 |     //    random, because symbols declared close together are assigned names
486 |     //    that are quite similar. With this heuristic, the output is more
487 |     //    compressible.
488 |     //    For instance, the output may look like:
489 |     //    var da = "..", ea = "..";
490 |     //    function fa() { .. } function ga() { .. }
491 | 
492 |     int numPendingAssignments = generatedNamesForAssignments.size();
493 |     for (int i = 0; i < numPendingAssignments;) {
494 |       SortedSet<Assignment> varsByOrderOfOccurrence =
495 |           new TreeSet<Assignment>(ORDER_OF_OCCURRENCE_COMPARATOR);
496 | 
497 |       // Add k number of Assignment to the set, where k is the number of
498 |       // generated names of the same length.
499 |       int len = generatedNamesForAssignments.get(i).length();
500 |       for (int j = i; j < numPendingAssignments
501 |           && generatedNamesForAssignments.get(j).length() == len; j++) {
502 |         varsByOrderOfOccurrence.add(pendingAssignments.get(j));
503 |       }
504 | 
505 |       // Now, make the assignments
506 |       for (Assignment a : varsByOrderOfOccurrence) {
507 |         finalizeNameAssignment(a, generatedNamesForAssignments.get(i));
508 |         ++i;
509 |       }
510 |     }
511 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)

Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0
315 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
316 |     }
317 |   }

===== M0907 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 117-170
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
117 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
118 |     switch (n.getType()) {
119 |       case Token.VAR:
120 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
121 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
122 |              child != null;) {
123 |           Node next = child.getNext();
124 |           declareVar(child);
125 |           child = next;
126 |         }
127 |         return;
128 | 
129 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
130 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
131 |           return;
132 |         }
133 | 
134 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
135 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
136 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
140 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
141 | 
142 |       case Token.CATCH:
143 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
144 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
145 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
146 |         // is the code block
147 | 
148 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
149 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
150 | 
151 |         declareVar(var);
152 |         scanVars(block, n);
153 |         return;  // only one child to scan
154 | 
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
157 |         break;
158 |     }
159 | 
160 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
161 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
162 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
163 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
164 |            child != null;) {
165 |         Node next = child.getNext();
166 |         scanVars(child, n);
167 |         child = next;
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

===== M0917 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReplaceCssNames.Traversal.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReplaceCssNames.java
Original range: 139-192
Documentation:

Method code:
139 |     @Override
140 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
141 |       if (n.getType() == Token.CALL &&
142 |           GET_CSS_NAME_FUNCTION.equals(n.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName())) {
143 |         int count = n.getChildCount();
144 |         Node first = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
145 |         switch (count) {
146 |           case 2:
147 |             // Replace the function call with the processed argument.
148 |             if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
149 |               processStringNode(t, first);
150 |               n.removeChild(first);
151 |               parent.replaceChild(n, first);
152 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
153 |             } else {
154 |               compiler.report(t.makeError(n, STRING_LITERAL_EXPECTED_ERROR,
155 |                   Token.name(first.getType())));
156 |             }
157 |             break;
158 | 
159 |           case 3:
160 |             // Replace function call with concatenation of two args.  It's
161 |             // assumed the first arg has already been processed.
162 | 
163 |             Node second = first.getNext();
164 | 
165 |             if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
166 |               compiler.report(t.makeError(
167 |                   n, UNEXPECTED_STRING_LITERAL_ERROR,
168 |                   first.getString(), second.getString()));
169 | 
170 |             } else if (second.getType() == Token.STRING) {
171 |               processStringNode(t, second);
172 |               n.removeChild(first);
173 |               Node replacement = new Node(Token.ADD, first,
174 |                   Node.newString("-" + second.getString())
175 |                       .copyInformationFrom(second))
176 |                   .copyInformationFrom(n);
177 |               replacement.setJSType(nativeStringType);
178 |               parent.replaceChild(n, replacement);
179 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
180 | 
181 |             } else {
182 |               compiler.report(t.makeError(n, STRING_LITERAL_EXPECTED_ERROR,
183 |                   Token.name(second.getType())));
184 |             }
185 |             break;
186 | 
187 |           default:
188 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(
189 |                 n, INVALID_NUM_ARGUMENTS_ERROR, String.valueOf(count)));
190 |         }
191 |       }
192 |     }

===== M0927 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 169-260
Documentation:

Method code:
169 |   @Override
170 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
171 |     switch (n.getType()) {
172 |       case Token.CALL:
173 |         boolean isExpr = parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT;
174 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
175 |         if (left.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
176 |           Node name = left.getFirstChild();
177 |           if (name.getType() == Token.NAME &&
178 |               GOOG.equals(name.getString())) {
179 |             // For the sake of simplicity, we report code changes
180 |             // when we see a provides/requires, and don't worry about
181 |             // reporting the change when we actually do the replacement.
182 |             String methodName = name.getNext().getString();
183 |             if ("base".equals(methodName)) {
184 |               processBaseClassCall(t, n);
185 |             } else if (!isExpr) {
186 |               // All other methods must be called in an EXPR.
187 |               break;
188 |             } else if ("require".equals(methodName)) {
189 |               processRequireCall(t, n, parent);
190 |             } else if ("provide".equals(methodName)) {
191 |               processProvideCall(t, n, parent);
192 |             } else if ("exportSymbol".equals(methodName)) {
193 |               Node arg = left.getNext();
194 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.STRING) {
195 |                 int dot = arg.getString().indexOf('.');
196 |                 if (dot == -1) {
197 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString());
198 |                 } else {
199 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString().substring(0, dot));
200 |                 }
201 |               }
202 |             } else if ("addDependency".equals(methodName)) {
203 |               CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
204 |               List<String> typeDecls =
205 |                   convention.identifyTypeDeclarationCall(n);
206 |               if (typeDecls != null) {
207 |                 for (String typeDecl : typeDecls) {
208 |                   compiler.getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(typeDecl);
209 |                 }
210 |               }
211 | 
212 |               // We can't modify parent, so just create a node that will
213 |               // get compiled out.
214 |               parent.replaceChild(n, Node.newNumber(0));
215 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
216 |             } else if ("setCssNameMapping".equals(methodName)) {
217 |               processSetCssNameMapping(t, n, parent);
218 |             }
219 |           }
220 |         }
221 |         break;
222 | 
223 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
224 |       case Token.NAME:
225 |         // If this is an assignment to a provided name, remove the provided
226 |         // object.
227 |         handleCandidateProvideDefinition(t, n, parent);
228 |         break;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
231 |         handleTypedefDefinition(t, n, parent);
232 |         break;
233 | 
234 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
235 |         // If this is a declaration of a provided named function, this is an
236 |         // error. Hosited functions will explode if the're provided.
237 |         if (t.inGlobalScope() &&
238 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
239 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
240 |           ProvidedName pn = providedNames.get(name);
241 |           if (pn != null) {
242 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, FUNCTION_NAMESPACE_ERROR, name));
243 |           }
244 |         }
245 |         break;
246 | 
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         trySimplifyNewDate(t, n, parent);
249 |         break;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.GETPROP:
252 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME &&
253 |             parent.getType() != Token.CALL &&
254 |             parent.getType() != Token.ASSIGN &&
255 |             "goog.base".equals(n.getQualifiedName())) {
256 |           reportBadBaseClassUse(t, n, "May only be called directly.");
257 |         }
258 |         break;
259 |     }
260 |   }

===== M0933 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.LoopInternal.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 313-348
Documentation:

Method code:
313 |     public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
314 |       Preconditions.checkState(!loopMutex, "Nested loops are forbidden");
315 |       loopMutex = true;
316 |       if (randomizeLoops) {
317 |         randomizePasses();
318 |       } else {
319 |         optimizePasses();
320 |       }
321 | 
322 |       try {
323 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Use a smarter algorithm that dynamically adjusts
324 |         // the order that passes are run in.
325 |         int count = 0;
326 |         out: do {
327 |           if (count++ > MAX_LOOPS) {
328 |             compiler.throwInternalError(OPTIMIZE_LOOP_ERROR, null);
329 |           }
330 | 
331 |           recentChange.reset();  // reset before this round of optimizations
332 | 
333 |           for (CompilerPass pass : myPasses) {
334 |             pass.process(externs, root);
335 |             if (hasHaltingErrors()) {
336 |               break out;
337 |             }
338 |           }
339 | 
340 |         } while (recentChange.hasCodeChanged() && !hasHaltingErrors());
341 | 
342 |         if (randomizeLoops) {
343 |           loopsRun.add(getPassOrder());
344 |         }
345 |       } finally {
346 |         loopMutex = false;
347 |       }
348 |     }

===== M0935 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1703-1736
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1703 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1704 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1705 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1706 |     }
1707 | 
1708 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1709 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1710 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1711 |       skipEOLs();
1712 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1713 |         next();
1714 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1715 |       }
1716 |     }
1717 | 
1718 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName);
1719 | 
1720 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1721 |       next();
1722 |       skipEOLs();
1723 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1724 |       if (memberType != null) {
1725 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1726 | 
1727 |         skipEOLs();
1728 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1729 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1730 |         }
1731 | 
1732 |         next();
1733 |       }
1734 |     }
1735 |     return typeNameNode;
1736 |   }

61 |   @Override
62 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
63 |     Node currentVersionOfNode = n;
64 |     boolean somethingChanged = false;
65 | 
66 |     do {
67 |       somethingChanged = false;
68 |       for (AbstractPeepholeOptimization optimization : peepholeOptimizations) {
69 |         Node newVersionOfNode =
70 |             optimization.optimizeSubtree(currentVersionOfNode);
71 | 
72 |         if (newVersionOfNode != currentVersionOfNode) {
73 |           somethingChanged = true;
74 | 
75 |           currentVersionOfNode = newVersionOfNode;
76 |         }
77 | 
78 |         if (currentVersionOfNode == null) {
79 |           return;
80 |         }
81 |       }
82 |     } while(somethingChanged);
83 |   }

203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);

===== M0947 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2449-2459
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2449 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2450 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2451 |     int len = s.length();
2452 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2453 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2454 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2455 |         return false;
2456 |       }
2457 |     }
2458 |     return true;
2459 |   }

===== M0984 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.OptimizeArgumentsArray.exitScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/OptimizeArgumentsArray.java
Original range: 113-140
Documentation:

Method code:
113 |   @Override
114 |   public void exitScope(NodeTraversal traversal) {
115 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(traversal);
116 | 
117 |     // This is the case when we are exiting the global scope where we had never
118 |     // collected argument access list. Since we do not perform this optimization
119 |     // for the global scope, we will skip this exit point.
120 |     if (currentArgumentsAccess == null) {
121 |       return;
122 |     }
123 | 
124 |     // Attempt to replace the argument access and if the AST has been change,
125 |     // report back to the compiler.
126 |     if (tryReplaceArguments(traversal.getScope())) {
127 |       traversal.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
128 |     }
129 | 
130 |     // After the attempt to replace the arguments. The currentArgumentsAccess
131 |     // is stale and as we exit the Scope, no longer holds all the access to the
132 |     // current scope anymore. We'll pop the access list from the outer scope
133 |     // and set it as currentArgumentsAcess if the outer scope is not the global
134 |     // scope.
135 |     if (!argumentsAccessStack.isEmpty()) {
136 |       currentArgumentsAccess = argumentsAccessStack.pop();
137 |     } else {
138 |       currentArgumentsAccess = null;
139 |     }
140 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RenameLabels.ProcessLabels.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RenameLabels.java
Original range: 136-159
Documentation:
/**
     * shouldTraverse is call when descending into the Node tree, so it is used
     * here to build the context for label renames.
     *
     * {@inheritDoc}
     */
Method code:
136 |     public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node node,
137 |         Node parent) {
138 |       if (node.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
139 |         // Determine the new name for this label.
140 |         LabelNamespace current = namespaceStack.peek();
141 |         int currentDepth = current.renameMap.size() + 1;
142 |         String name = node.getFirstChild().getString();
143 | 
144 |         // Store the context for this label name.
145 |         LabelInfo li = new LabelInfo(currentDepth);
146 |         Preconditions.checkState(!current.renameMap.containsKey(name));
147 |         current.renameMap.put(name, li);
148 | 
149 |         // Create a new name, if needed, for this depth.
150 |         if (names.size() < currentDepth) {
151 |           names.add(nameSupplier.get());
152 |         }
153 | 
154 |         String newName = getNameForId(currentDepth);
155 |         compiler.addToDebugLog("label renamed: " + name + " => " + newName);
156 |       }
157 | 
158 |       return true;
159 |     }

===== M0998 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-88
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         }
70 | 
71 |         if (info != null) {
72 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
73 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
74 | 
75 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
76 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
77 |             if (guard != null) {
78 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
79 |               if (newLevel != null) {
80 |                 return newLevel;
81 |               }
82 |             }
83 |           }
84 |         }
85 |       }
86 |     }
87 |     return null;
88 |   }
