171 |     @Override
172 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
173 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
174 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
175 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
176 |           return;
177 |         }
178 | 
179 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
180 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top level variable.
181 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
182 |         if (var != null) {
183 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
184 |         }
185 | 
186 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
187 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
188 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
189 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
190 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
191 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
192 |           if (assertOnChange) {
193 |             String name = n.getString();
194 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
195 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
196 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
197 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
198 |           }
199 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
200 |         }
201 |       }
202 |     }

===== M1002 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.CodeBuilder.append(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1499-1518
Documentation:
/** Appends the given string to the text buffer. */
Method code:
1499 |     CodeBuilder append(String str) {
1500 |       sb.append(str);
1501 | 
1502 |       // Adjust the line and column information for the new text.
1503 |       int index = -1;
1504 |       int lastIndex = index;
1505 |       while ((index = str.indexOf('\n', index + 1)) >= 0) {
1506 |         ++lineCount;
1507 |         lastIndex = index;
1508 |       }
1509 | 
1510 |       if (lastIndex == -1) {
1511 |         // No new lines, append the new characters added.
1512 |         colCount += str.length();
1513 |       } else {
1514 |         colCount = str.length() - (lastIndex + 1);
1515 |       }
1516 | 
1517 |       return this;
1518 |     }

===== M1010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.tryOptimizeBlock(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeRemoveDeadCode.java
Original range: 446-473
Documentation:
/**
   * Try removing unneeded block nodes and their useless children
   */
Method code:
446 |   Node tryOptimizeBlock(Node n) {
447 |     // Remove any useless children
448 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; ) {
449 |       Node next = c.getNext();  // save c.next, since 'c' may be removed
450 |       if (!mayHaveSideEffects(c) && !c.isSyntheticBlock()) {
451 |         // TODO(johnlenz): determine what this is actually removing. Candidates
452 |         //    include: EMPTY nodes, control structures without children
453 |         //    (removing infinite loops), empty try blocks.  What else?
454 |         n.removeChild(c);  // lazy kids
455 |         reportCodeChange();
456 |       } else {
457 |         tryOptimizeConditionalAfterAssign(c);
458 |       }
459 |       c = next;
460 |     }
461 | 
462 |     if (n.isSyntheticBlock() ||  n.getParent() == null) {
463 |       return n;
464 |     }
465 | 
466 |     // Try to remove the block.
467 |     if (NodeUtil.tryMergeBlock(n)) {
468 |       reportCodeChange();
469 |       return null;
470 |     }
471 | 
472 |     return n;
473 |   }

===== M1012 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 786-800
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
786 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
787 |     switch (n.getType()) {
788 |       case Token.DO:
789 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
790 |       case Token.FOR:
791 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
792 |           return n;
793 |         }
794 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
795 |       case Token.LABEL:
796 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
797 |       default:
798 |         return n;
799 |     }
800 |   }

===== M1018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

===== M1022 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 150-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
150 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
151 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
152 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
153 |       case Token.BLOCK:
154 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
155 |       case Token.TRY:
156 |       case Token.FINALLY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M1034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.createDefineOrTweakReplacements(List<String>,CompilerOptions,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 1077-1136
Documentation:
/**
   * Create a map of constant names to constant values from a textual
   * description of the map.
   *
   * @param definitions A list of overriding definitions for defines in
   *     the form <name>[=<val>], where <val> is a number, boolean, or
   *     single-quoted string without single quotes.
   */
Method code:
1077 |   @VisibleForTesting
1078 |   static void createDefineOrTweakReplacements(List<String> definitions,
1079 |       CompilerOptions options, boolean tweaks) {
1080 |     // Parse the definitions
1081 |     for (String override : definitions) {
1082 |       String[] assignment = override.split("=", 2);
1083 |       String defName = assignment[0];
1084 | 
1085 |       if (defName.length() > 0) {
1086 |         String defValue = assignment.length == 1 ? "true" : assignment[1];
1087 | 
1088 |         boolean isTrue = defValue.equals("true");
1089 |         boolean isFalse = defValue.equals("false");
1090 |         if (isTrue || isFalse) {
1091 |           if (tweaks) {
1092 |             options.setTweakToBooleanLiteral(defName, isTrue);
1093 |           } else {
1094 |             options.setDefineToBooleanLiteral(defName, isTrue);
1095 |           }
1096 |           continue;
1097 |         } else if (defValue.length() > 1
1098 |             && ((defValue.charAt(0) == '\'' &&
1099 |                 defValue.charAt(defValue.length() - 1) == '\'')
1100 |                 || (defValue.charAt(0) == '\"' &&
1101 |                     defValue.charAt(defValue.length() - 1) == '\"'))) {
1102 |           // If the value starts and ends with a single quote,
1103 |           // we assume that it's a string.
1104 |           String maybeStringVal =
1105 |               defValue.substring(1, defValue.length() - 1);
1106 |           if (maybeStringVal.indexOf(defValue.charAt(0)) == -1) {
1107 |             if (tweaks) {
1108 |               options.setTweakToStringLiteral(defName, maybeStringVal);
1109 |             } else {
1110 |               options.setDefineToStringLiteral(defName, maybeStringVal);
1111 |             }
1112 |             continue;
1113 |           }
1114 |         } else {
1115 |           try {
1116 |             double value = Double.parseDouble(defValue);
1117 |             if (tweaks) {
1118 |               options.setTweakToDoubleLiteral(defName, value);
1119 |             } else {
1120 |               options.setDefineToDoubleLiteral(defName, value);
1121 |             }
1122 |             continue;
1123 |           } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
1124 |             // do nothing, it will be caught at the end
1125 |           }
1126 |         }
1127 |       }
1128 | 
1129 |       if (tweaks) {
1130 |         throw new RuntimeException(
1131 |             "--tweak flag syntax invalid: " + override);
1132 |       }
1133 |       throw new RuntimeException(
1134 |           "--define flag syntax invalid: " + override);
1135 |     }
1136 |   }

===== M1049 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.OperaCompoundAssignFix.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/OperaCompoundAssignFix.java
Original range: 81-120
Documentation:

Method code:
81 |   @Override
82 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
83 |     if (!NodeUtil.isName(n)) {
84 |       return;
85 |     }
86 | 
87 |     if (!NodeUtil.isGet(parent)) {
88 |       return;
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     boolean nested = false;
92 |     boolean reassign = false;
93 |     Node lastAssign = null;
94 |     Node prevParent = n;
95 | 
96 |     while (!(NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
97 |              NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent))) {
98 |       if (NodeUtil.isAssign(parent) &&
99 |           NodeUtil.isName(parent.getFirstChild()) &&
100 |           parent.getFirstChild().getString().equals(n.getString()) &&
101 |           nested) {
102 |         reassign = true;
103 |         break;
104 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
105 |           parent.getLastChild() == prevParent) {
106 |         if (lastAssign == null) {
107 |           nested = true;
108 |         }
109 |         lastAssign = parent;
110 |       }
111 |       prevParent = parent;
112 |       parent = parent.getParent();
113 |     }
114 | 
115 |     if (!(reassign && nested)) {
116 |       return;
117 |     }
118 | 
119 |     applyWorkAround(parent, t);
120 |   }

===== M1071 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 888-915
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
888 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

===== M1077 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M1082 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 832-874
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
832 |   @Override
833 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
834 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
835 |       return true;
836 |     }
837 | 
838 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
839 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
840 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
841 |         return true;
842 |       }
843 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
844 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
845 |         return false;
846 |       }
847 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
848 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
849 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
850 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
851 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
852 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
853 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
854 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
855 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
856 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
857 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
858 | 
859 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
860 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
861 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
862 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
863 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
864 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
865 | 
866 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
867 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
868 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
869 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
870 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
871 |     }
872 | 
873 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
874 |   }

===== M1083 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

===== M1086 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.writeOutput(Appendable,Compiler,String,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 564-595
Documentation:
/**
   * Writes code to an output stream, optionally wrapping it in an arbitrary
   * wrapper that contains a placeholder where the code should be inserted.
   */
Method code:
564 |   static void writeOutput(Appendable out, Compiler compiler, String code,
565 |       String wrapper, String codePlaceholder) throws IOException {
566 |     int pos = wrapper.indexOf(codePlaceholder);
567 |     if (pos != -1) {
568 |       String prefix = "";
569 | 
570 |       if (pos > 0) {
571 |         prefix = wrapper.substring(0, pos);
572 |         out.append(prefix);
573 |       }
574 | 
575 |       out.append(code);
576 | 
577 |       int suffixStart = pos + codePlaceholder.length();
578 |       if (suffixStart != wrapper.length()) {
579 |         // Something after placeholder?
580 |         out.append(wrapper.substring(suffixStart));
581 |       }
582 |       // Make sure we always end output with a line feed.
583 |       out.append('\n');
584 | 
585 |       // If we have a source map, adjust its offsets to match
586 |       // the code WITHIN the wrapper.
587 |       if (compiler != null && compiler.getSourceMap() != null) {
588 |         compiler.getSourceMap().setWrapperPrefix(prefix);
589 |       }
590 | 
591 |     } else {
592 |       out.append(code);
593 |       out.append('\n');
594 |     }
595 |   }

===== M1096 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ShadowVariables.DoShadowVariables.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ShadowVariables.java
Original range: 175-226
Documentation:

Method code:
175 |     @Override
176 |     public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
177 |       Scope s = t.getScope();
178 |       if (!s.isLocal()) {
179 |         return;
180 |       }
181 | 
182 |       // Since we don't shadow global, there is nothing to be done in the
183 |       // first immediate local scope as well.
184 |       if (s.getParent().isGlobal()) {
185 |         return;
186 |       }
187 | 
188 |       for (Iterator<Var> vars = s.getVars(); vars.hasNext();) {
189 |         Var var = vars.next();
190 | 
191 |         // Don't shadow variables that is bleed-out to fix an IE bug.
192 |         if (var.isBleedingFunction()) {
193 |           continue;
194 |         }
195 | 
196 |         // Don't shadow an exported local.
197 |         if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(var.name, s.isLocal())) {
198 |           continue;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         // Try to look for the best shadow for the current candidate.
202 |         Assignment bestShadow = findBestShadow(s, var);
203 |         if (bestShadow == null) {
204 |           continue;
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         // The name assignment being shadowed.
208 |         Assignment localAssignment = assignments.get(var.getName());
209 | 
210 |         // Only shadow if this increases the number of occurrences of the
211 |         // shadowed variable.
212 |         if (bestShadow.count < localAssignment.count) {
213 |           continue; // Hope the next local variable would have a smaller count.
214 |         }
215 | 
216 |         doShadow(localAssignment, bestShadow, var);
217 | 
218 |         if (oldPseudoNameMap != null) {
219 |           String targetPseudoName =
220 |             oldPseudoNameMap.get(s.getVar(bestShadow.oldName).nameNode);
221 |           for (Node use : varToNameUsage.get(var)) {
222 |             deltaPseudoNameMap.put(use, targetPseudoName);
223 |           }
224 |         }
225 |       }
226 |     }

/**

===== M1102 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 723-866
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
723 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
724 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
725 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
726 |     // that we know to be safe
727 |     switch (n.getType()) {
728 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
729 |       case Token.AND:
730 |       case Token.BLOCK:
731 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
732 |       case Token.HOOK:
733 |       case Token.IF:
734 |       case Token.IN:
735 |       case Token.LP:
736 |       case Token.NUMBER:
737 |       case Token.OR:
738 |       case Token.THIS:
739 |       case Token.TRUE:
740 |       case Token.FALSE:
741 |       case Token.NULL:
742 |       case Token.STRING:
743 |       case Token.SWITCH:
744 |       case Token.TRY:
745 |       case Token.EMPTY:
746 |         break;
747 | 
748 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
749 |       case Token.THROW:
750 |         return true;
751 | 
752 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
753 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
754 |           return true;
755 |         }
756 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
757 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
758 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
759 |             return true;
760 |           }
761 |         }
762 |         return false;
763 | 
764 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
765 |       case Token.REGEXP:
766 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
767 |           return true;
768 |         }
769 |         break;
770 | 
771 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
772 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
773 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
774 |           return true;
775 |         }
776 |         break;
777 | 
778 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
779 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
780 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
781 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
782 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
783 | 
784 |       case Token.NEW:
785 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
786 |           return true;
787 |         }
788 | 
789 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
790 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
791 |           // side-effects
792 |           break;
793 |         }
794 |         return true;
795 | 
796 |       case Token.CALL:
797 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
798 |         // side effect property set.
799 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
800 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
801 |           // side-effects
802 |           break;
803 |         }
804 |         return true;
805 | 
806 |       default:
807 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
808 |           break;
809 |         }
810 | 
811 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
812 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
813 |           if (isName(assignTarget)) {
814 |             return true;
815 |           }
816 | 
817 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
818 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
819 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
820 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
821 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
822 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
823 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
824 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
825 |             return true;
826 |           }
827 | 
828 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
829 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
830 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
831 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
832 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
833 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
834 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
835 |               return false;
836 |             }
837 | 
838 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
839 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
840 |             // other objects.
841 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
842 |             // side-effect.
843 |             while (isGet(current)) {
844 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
845 |             }
846 | 
847 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
848 |           } else {
849 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
850 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
851 |             // preserve existing behavior.
852 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
853 |           }
854 |         }
855 | 
856 |         return true;
857 |     }
858 | 
859 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
860 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
861 |         return true;
862 |       }
863 |     }
864 | 
865 |     return false;
866 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/GraphReachability.java
Original range: 54-59
Documentation:

===== M1118 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RenameVars.ProcessVars.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RenameVars.java
Original range: 213-284
Documentation:

Method code:
213 |     @Override
214 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
215 |       if (n.getType() != Token.NAME) {
216 |         return;
217 |       }
218 | 
219 |       String name = n.getString();
220 | 
221 |       // Ignore anonymous functions
222 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
223 |         return;
224 |       }
225 | 
226 |       // Is this local or Global?
227 |       // Bleeding functions should be treated as part of their outer
228 |       // scope, because IE has bugs in how it handles bleeding
229 |       // functions.
230 |       Scope.Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name);
231 |       boolean local = (var != null) && var.isLocal() &&
232 |           (!var.scope.getParent().isGlobal() ||
233 |            !var.isBleedingFunction());
234 | 
235 |       // Are we renaming global variables?
236 |       if (!local && localRenamingOnly) {
237 |         reservedNames.add(name);
238 |         return;
239 |       }
240 | 
241 |       // Are we renaming function expression names?
242 |       if (preserveFunctionExpressionNames && var != null
243 |           && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(var.getParentNode())) {
244 |         reservedNames.add(name);
245 |         return;
246 |       }
247 | 
248 |       // Check if we can rename this.
249 |       if (!okToRenameVar(name, local)) {
250 |         if (local) {
251 |           // Blindly de-uniquify for the Prototype library for issue 103.
252 |           String newName = MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.ContextualRenameInverter
253 |               .getOrginalName(name);
254 |           if (!newName.equals(name)) {
255 |             n.setString(newName);
256 |           }
257 |         }
258 |         return;
259 |       }
260 | 
261 |       if (isExternsPass_) {
262 |         // Keep track of extern globals.
263 |         if (!local) {
264 |           externNames.add(name);
265 |         }
266 |         return;
267 |       }
268 | 
269 |       if (pseudoNameMap != null) {
270 |         recordPseudoName(n);
271 |       }
272 | 
273 |       if (local) {
274 |         // Local var: assign a new name
275 |         String tempName = LOCAL_VAR_PREFIX + getLocalVarIndex(var);
276 |         incCount(tempName);
277 |         localNameNodes.add(n);
278 |         n.setString(tempName);
279 |       } else if (var != null) { // Not an extern
280 |         // If it's global, increment global count
281 |         incCount(name);
282 |         globalNameNodes.add(n);
283 |       }
284 |     }

===== M1126 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 607-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

===== M1137 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeCollectPropertyAssignments.optimizeSubtree(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeCollectPropertyAssignments.java
Original range: 34-82
Documentation:

Method code:
34 |   @Override
35 |   Node optimizeSubtree(Node subtree) {
36 |     if (subtree.getType() != Token.SCRIPT
37 |         && subtree.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
38 |       return subtree;
39 |     }
40 | 
41 |     boolean codeChanged = false;
42 | 
43 |     // Look for variable declarations or simple assignments
44 |     // and start processing there.
45 |     for (Node child = subtree.getFirstChild();
46 |          child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
47 |       if (child.getType() != Token.VAR && !NodeUtil.isExprAssign(child)) {
48 |         continue;
49 |       }
50 |       if (!isPropertyAssignmentToName(child.getNext())) {
51 |         // Quick check to see if there's anything to collapse.
52 |         continue;
53 |       }
54 | 
55 |       Preconditions.checkState(child.hasOneChild());
56 |       Node name = getName(child);
57 |       if (name.getType() != Token.NAME) {
58 |         // The assignment target is not a simple name.
59 |         continue;
60 |       }
61 |       Node value = getValue(child);
62 |       if (value == null || !isInterestingValue(value)) {
63 |         // No initializer or not an Object or Array literal.
64 |         continue;
65 |       }
66 | 
67 |       Node propertyCandidate;
68 |       while ((propertyCandidate = child.getNext()) != null) {
69 |         // This does not infinitely loop because collectProperty always
70 |         // removes propertyCandidate from its parent when it returns true.
71 |         if (!collectProperty(propertyCandidate, name.getString(), value)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |         codeChanged = true;
75 |       }
76 |     }
77 | 
78 |     if (codeChanged) {
79 |       reportCodeChange();
80 |     }
81 |     return subtree;
82 |   }

===== M1139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 86-735
Documentation:

Method code:
86 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
87 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
88 |       return;
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     int type = n.getType();
92 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
93 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
94 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
95 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
96 | 
97 |     // Handle all binary operators
98 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
99 |       Preconditions.checkState(
100 |           childCount == 2,
101 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
102 |           opstr, childCount);
103 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
104 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
105 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
106 | 
107 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
108 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
109 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
110 | 
111 |       // Handle associativity.
112 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
113 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
114 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
115 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
116 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
117 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
118 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
119 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
120 |       } else {
121 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
122 |       }
123 |       return;
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
127 | 
128 |     switch (type) {
129 |       case Token.TRY: {
130 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
131 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
132 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
133 | 
134 |         add("try");
135 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 | 
137 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
138 |         // isn't a catch block
139 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
140 |         if (catchblock != null) {
141 |           add(catchblock);
142 |         }
143 | 
144 |         if (childCount == 3) {
145 |           add("finally");
146 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
147 |         }
148 |         break;
149 |       }
150 | 
151 |       case Token.CATCH:
152 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
153 |         add("catch(");
154 |         add(first);
155 |         add(")");
156 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
157 |         break;
158 | 
159 |       case Token.THROW:
160 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
161 |         add("throw");
162 |         add(first);
163 | 
164 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
165 |         // parse this.
166 |         cc.endStatement(true);
167 |         break;
168 | 
169 |       case Token.RETURN:
170 |         add("return");
171 |         if (childCount == 1) {
172 |           add(first);
173 |         } else {
174 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
175 |         }
176 |         cc.endStatement();
177 |         break;
178 | 
179 |       case Token.VAR:
180 |         if (first != null) {
181 |           add("var ");
182 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
183 |         }
184 |         break;
185 | 
186 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
187 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
188 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
189 |         break;
190 | 
191 |       case Token.NAME:
192 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
193 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
194 |         } else {
195 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
196 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
197 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
198 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
199 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
200 |           } else {
201 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
202 |             // precedence.
203 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
204 |           }
205 |         }
206 |         break;
207 | 
208 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
209 |         add("[");
210 |         addArrayList(first);
211 |         add("]");
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.LP:
215 |         add("(");
216 |         addList(first);
217 |         add(")");
218 |         break;
219 | 
220 |       case Token.COMMA:
221 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
222 |         addList(first, false, context);
223 |         break;
224 | 
225 |       case Token.NUMBER:
226 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
227 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
228 |         break;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
231 |       case Token.VOID:
232 |       case Token.NOT:
233 |       case Token.BITNOT:
234 |       case Token.POS: {
235 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
236 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
237 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
238 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
239 |         break;
240 |       }
241 | 
242 |       case Token.NEG: {
243 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
244 | 
245 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
246 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
247 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
248 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
249 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
250 |         } else {
251 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
252 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
253 |         }
254 | 
255 |         break;
256 |       }
257 | 
258 |       case Token.HOOK: {
259 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
260 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
261 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
262 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
263 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
264 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
265 |         addExpr(last, 1);
266 |         break;
267 |       }
268 | 
269 |       case Token.REGEXP:
270 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
271 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
272 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
273 |         }
274 | 
275 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
276 | 
277 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
278 |         if (childCount == 2) {
279 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
280 |         } else {
281 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
282 |           add(regexp);
283 |         }
284 |         break;
285 | 
286 |       case Token.GET_REF:
287 |         add(first);
288 |         break;
289 | 
290 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
292 |         add(first);
293 |         add(".");
294 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
295 |         break;
296 | 
297 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
298 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
299 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
300 |         }
301 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
302 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
303 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
304 |           add("(");
305 |         }
306 | 
307 |         add("function");
308 |         add(first);
309 | 
310 |         add(first.getNext());
311 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
312 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
313 | 
314 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
315 |           add(")");
316 |         }
317 |         break;
318 | 
319 |       case Token.GET:
320 |       case Token.SET:
321 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
322 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
323 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
324 | 
325 |         // Get methods are unnamed
326 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
327 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
328 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
329 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
330 |           add("get ");
331 |         } else {
332 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
333 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
334 |           add("set ");
335 |         }
336 | 
337 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
338 |         String name = n.getString();
339 |         Node fn = first;
340 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
341 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
342 | 
343 |         // Add the property name.
344 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
345 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
346 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
347 |             // unicode escaped.
348 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
349 |           add(name);
350 |         } else {
351 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
352 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
353 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
354 |             cc.addNumber(d);
355 |           } else {
356 |             addJsString(n.getString());
357 |           }
358 |         }
359 | 
360 |         add(parameters);
361 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
362 |         break;
363 | 
364 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
365 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
366 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
367 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
368 |         }
369 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
370 |         if (preserveBlock) {
371 |           cc.beginBlock();
372 |         }
373 | 
374 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
375 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
376 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
377 |                 !preserveBlock &&
378 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
379 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
380 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
381 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
382 | 
383 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
384 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
385 |             cc.endStatement();
386 |           }
387 | 
388 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
389 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
390 |           }
391 | 
392 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
393 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
394 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
395 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
396 |           }
397 |         }
398 |         if (preserveBlock) {
399 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
400 |         }
401 |         break;
402 |       }
403 | 
404 |       case Token.FOR:
405 |         if (childCount == 4) {
406 |           add("for(");
407 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
408 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
409 |           } else {
410 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
411 |           }
412 |           add(";");
413 |           add(first.getNext());
414 |           add(";");
415 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
416 |           add(")");
417 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
418 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
419 |         } else {
420 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
421 |           add("for(");
422 |           add(first);
423 |           add("in");
424 |           add(first.getNext());
425 |           add(")");
426 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
427 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
428 |         }
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.DO:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("do");
434 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
435 |         add("while(");
436 |         add(last);
437 |         add(")");
438 |         cc.endStatement();
439 |         break;
440 | 
441 |       case Token.WHILE:
442 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
443 |         add("while(");
444 |         add(first);
445 |         add(")");
446 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
447 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
448 |         break;
449 | 
450 |       case Token.EMPTY:
451 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
452 |         break;
453 | 
454 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
455 |         Preconditions.checkState(
456 |             childCount == 2,
457 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
458 |         Preconditions.checkState(
459 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
460 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
461 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
462 |         if (needsParens) {
463 |           add("(");
464 |         }
465 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
466 |         if (needsParens) {
467 |           add(")");
468 |         }
469 |         add(".");
470 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
471 |         break;
472 |       }
473 | 
474 |       case Token.GETELEM:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(
476 |             childCount == 2,
477 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
478 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
479 |         add("[");
480 |         add(first.getNext());
481 |         add("]");
482 |         break;
483 | 
484 |       case Token.WITH:
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
486 |         add("with(");
487 |         add(first);
488 |         add(")");
489 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
490 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       case Token.INC:
494 |       case Token.DEC: {
495 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
496 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
497 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
498 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
499 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
500 |         if (postProp != 0) {
501 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
502 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
503 |         } else {
504 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
505 |           add(first);
506 |         }
507 |         break;
508 |       }
509 | 
510 |       case Token.CALL:
511 |         // We have two special cases here:
512 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
513 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
514 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
515 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
516 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
517 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
518 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
519 |         // that must be preserved.
520 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
521 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
522 |           add("(0,");
523 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
524 |           add(")");
525 |         } else {
526 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
527 |         }
528 |         add("(");
529 |         addList(first.getNext());
530 |         add(")");
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.IF:
534 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
535 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
536 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
537 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
538 |           cc.beginBlock();
539 |         }
540 | 
541 |         add("if(");
542 |         add(first);
543 |         add(")");
544 | 
545 |         if (hasElse) {
546 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
547 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
548 |           add("else");
549 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
550 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
551 |         } else {
552 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
553 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
554 |         }
555 | 
556 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
557 |           cc.endBlock();
558 |         }
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.NULL:
562 |       case Token.THIS:
563 |       case Token.FALSE:
564 |       case Token.TRUE:
565 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
566 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
571 |         add("continue");
572 |         if (childCount == 1) {
573 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
574 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
575 |           }
576 |           add(" ");
577 |           add(first);
578 |         }
579 |         cc.endStatement();
580 |         break;
581 | 
582 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
583 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
584 |         add("debugger");
585 |         cc.endStatement();
586 |         break;
587 | 
588 |       case Token.BREAK:
589 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
590 |         add("break");
591 |         if (childCount == 1) {
592 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
593 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
594 |           }
595 |           add(" ");
596 |           add(first);
597 |         }
598 |         cc.endStatement();
599 |         break;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
602 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
603 | 
604 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
605 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
606 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
607 |         cc.endStatement();
608 |         break;
609 | 
610 |       case Token.NEW:
611 |         add("new ");
612 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
613 | 
614 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
615 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
616 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
617 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING:
632 |         if (childCount !=
633 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
634 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
635 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
636 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
637 |         }
638 |         addJsString(n.getString());
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.DELPROP:
642 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
643 |         add("delete ");
644 |         add(first);
645 |         break;
646 | 
647 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
648 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
649 |         if (needsParens) {
650 |           add("(");
651 |         }
652 |         add("{");
653 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
654 |           if (c != first) {
655 |             cc.listSeparator();
656 |           }
657 | 
658 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
659 |             add(c);
660 |           } else {
661 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.getType() == Token.STRING);
662 |             String key = c.getString();
663 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
664 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
665 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
666 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
667 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
668 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
669 |                 // were unicode escaped.
670 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
671 |               add(key);
672 |             } else {
673 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
674 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
675 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
676 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
677 |               } else {
678 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
679 |               }
680 |             }
681 |             add(":");
682 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
683 |           }
684 |         }
685 |         add("}");
686 |         if (needsParens) {
687 |           add(")");
688 |         }
689 |         break;
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       case Token.SWITCH:
693 |         add("switch(");
694 |         add(first);
695 |         add(")");
696 |         cc.beginBlock();
697 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
698 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
699 |         break;
700 | 
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
703 |         add("case ");
704 |         add(first);
705 |         addCaseBody(last);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
709 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
710 |         add("default");
711 |         addCaseBody(first);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.LABEL:
715 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
716 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
717 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
718 |         }
719 |         add(first);
720 |         add(":");
721 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
722 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
726 |       // ignored for our purposes.
727 |       case Token.SETNAME:
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       default:
731 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
732 |     }
733 | 
734 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
735 |   }

===== M1152 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldGetProp(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 1267-1302
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold array-length. e.g [1, 2, 3].length ==> 3, [x, y].length ==> 2
   */
Method code:
1267 |   private Node tryFoldGetProp(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
1268 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.getType() == Token.GETPROP);
1269 | 
1270 |     if (left.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1271 |       return tryFoldObjectPropAccess(n, left, right);
1272 |     }
1273 | 
1274 |     if (right.getType() == Token.STRING &&
1275 |         right.getString().equals("length")) {
1276 |       int knownLength = -1;
1277 |       switch (left.getType()) {
1278 |         case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1279 |           if (mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
1280 |             // Nope, can't fold this, without handling the side-effects.
1281 |             return n;
1282 |           }
1283 |           knownLength = left.getChildCount();
1284 |           break;
1285 |         case Token.STRING:
1286 |           knownLength = left.getString().length();
1287 |           break;
1288 |         default:
1289 |           // Not a foldable case, forget it.
1290 |           return n;
1291 |       }
1292 | 
1293 |       Preconditions.checkState(knownLength != -1);
1294 |       Node lengthNode = Node.newNumber(knownLength);
1295 |       n.getParent().replaceChild(n, lengthNode);
1296 |       reportCodeChange();
1297 | 
1298 |       return lengthNode;
1299 |     }
1300 | 
1301 |     return n;
1302 |   }

===== M1156 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M1160 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.toSource(CodeBuilder,int,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1393-1452
Documentation:
/**
   * Writes out js code from a root node. If printing input delimiters, this
   * method will attach a comment to the start of the text indicating which
   * input the output derived from. If there were any preserve annotations
   * within the root's source, they will also be printed in a block comment
   * at the beginning of the output.
   */
Method code:
1393 |   public void toSource(final CodeBuilder cb,
1394 |                        final int inputSeqNum,
1395 |                        final Node root) {
1396 |     runInCompilerThread(new Callable<Void>() {
1397 |       public Void call() throws Exception {
1398 |         if (options.printInputDelimiter) {
1399 |           if ((cb.getLength() > 0) && !cb.endsWith("\n")) {
1400 |             cb.append("\n");  // Make sure that the label starts on a new line
1401 |           }
1402 |           Preconditions.checkState(root.getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
1403 | 
1404 |           String delimiter = options.inputDelimiter;
1405 | 
1406 |           String sourceName = (String)root.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
1407 |           Preconditions.checkState(sourceName != null);
1408 |           Preconditions.checkState(!sourceName.isEmpty());
1409 | 
1410 |           delimiter = delimiter.replaceAll("%name%", sourceName)
1411 |             .replaceAll("%num%", String.valueOf(inputSeqNum));
1412 | 
1413 |           cb.append(delimiter)
1414 |             .append("\n");
1415 |         }
1416 |         if (root.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
1417 |             root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null) {
1418 |           cb.append("/*\n")
1419 |             .append(root.getJSDocInfo().getLicense())
1420 |             .append("*/\n");
1421 |         }
1422 | 
1423 |         // If there is a valid source map, then indicate to it that the current
1424 |         // root node's mappings are offset by the given string builder buffer.
1425 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null) {
1426 |           sourceMap.setStartingPosition(
1427 |               cb.getLineIndex(), cb.getColumnIndex());
1428 |         }
1429 | 
1430 |         // if LanguageMode is ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT, only print 'use strict'
1431 |         // for the first input file
1432 |         String code = toSource(root, sourceMap);
1433 |         if (!code.isEmpty()) {
1434 |           cb.append(code);
1435 | 
1436 |           // In order to avoid parse ambiguity when files are concatenated
1437 |           // together, all files should end in a semi-colon. Do a quick
1438 |           // heuristic check if there's an obvious semi-colon already there.
1439 |           int length = code.length();
1440 |           char lastChar = code.charAt(length - 1);
1441 |           char secondLastChar = length >= 2 ?
1442 |               code.charAt(length - 2) : '\0';
1443 |           boolean hasSemiColon = lastChar == ';' ||
1444 |               (lastChar == '\n' && secondLastChar == ';');
1445 |           if (!hasSemiColon) {
1446 |             cb.append(";");
1447 |           }
1448 |         }
1449 |         return null;
1450 |       }
1451 |     });
1452 |   }

===== M1169 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1511-1546
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1511 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1512 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1513 | 
1514 |     skipEOLs();
1515 | 
1516 |     boolean restArg = false;
1517 |     token = next();
1518 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1519 |       token = next();
1520 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1521 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1522 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1523 |       }
1524 |       restArg = true;
1525 |     }
1526 | 
1527 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1528 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1529 |       skipEOLs();
1530 |       if (restArg) {
1531 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1532 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1533 |         next();
1534 |         skipEOLs();
1535 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1536 |       }
1537 | 
1538 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1539 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1540 |       } else {
1541 |         next();
1542 |       }
1543 |     }
1544 | 
1545 |     return typeNode;
1546 |   }
