===== M1177 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefaultPassConfig.getOptimizations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefaultPassConfig.java
Original range: 313-634
Documentation:

Method code:
313 |   @Override
314 |   protected List<PassFactory> getOptimizations() {
315 |     List<PassFactory> passes = Lists.newArrayList();
316 | 
317 |     // TODO(nicksantos): The order of these passes makes no sense, and needs
318 |     // to be re-arranged.
319 | 
320 |     if (options.runtimeTypeCheck) {
321 |       passes.add(runtimeTypeCheck);
322 |     }
323 | 
324 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("beforeStandardOptimizations"));
325 | 
326 |     if (!options.idGenerators.isEmpty()) {
327 |       passes.add(replaceIdGenerators);
328 |     }
329 | 
330 |     // Optimizes references to the arguments variable.
331 |     if (options.optimizeArgumentsArray) {
332 |       passes.add(optimizeArgumentsArray);
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     // Abstract method removal works best on minimally modified code, and also
336 |     // only needs to run once.
337 |     if (options.closurePass &&
338 |         (options.removeAbstractMethods || options.removeClosureAsserts)) {
339 |       passes.add(closureCodeRemoval);
340 |     }
341 | 
342 |     // Collapsing properties can undo constant inlining, so we do this before
343 |     // the main optimization loop.
344 |     if (options.collapseProperties) {
345 |       passes.add(collapseProperties);
346 |     }
347 | 
348 |     // ReplaceStrings runs after CollapseProperties in order to simplify
349 |     // pulling in values of constants defined in enums structures.
350 |     if (!options.replaceStringsFunctionDescriptions.isEmpty()) {
351 |       passes.add(replaceStrings);
352 |     }
353 | 
354 |     // Tighten types based on actual usage.
355 |     if (options.tightenTypes) {
356 |       passes.add(tightenTypesBuilder);
357 |     }
358 | 
359 |     // Property disambiguation should only run once and needs to be done
360 |     // soon after type checking, both so that it can make use of type
361 |     // information and so that other passes can take advantage of the renamed
362 |     // properties.
363 |     if (options.disambiguateProperties) {
364 |       passes.add(disambiguateProperties);
365 |     }
366 | 
367 |     if (options.computeFunctionSideEffects) {
368 |       passes.add(markPureFunctions);
369 |     } else if (options.markNoSideEffectCalls) {
370 |       // TODO(user) The properties that this pass adds to CALL and NEW
371 |       // AST nodes increase the AST's in-memory size.  Given that we are
372 |       // already running close to our memory limits, we could run into
373 |       // trouble if we end up using the @nosideeffects annotation a lot
374 |       // or compute @nosideeffects annotations by looking at function
375 |       // bodies.  It should be easy to propagate @nosideeffects
376 |       // annotations as part of passes that depend on this property and
377 |       // store the result outside the AST (which would allow garbage
378 |       // collection once the pass is done).
379 |       passes.add(markNoSideEffectCalls);
380 |     }
381 | 
382 |     if (options.chainCalls) {
383 |       passes.add(chainCalls);
384 |     }
385 | 
386 |     // Constant checking must be done after property collapsing because
387 |     // property collapsing can introduce new constants (e.g. enum values).
388 |     if (options.inlineConstantVars) {
389 |       passes.add(checkConsts);
390 |     }
391 | 
392 |     // The Caja library adds properties to Object.prototype, which breaks
393 |     // most for-in loops.  This adds a check to each loop that skips
394 |     // any property matching /___$/.
395 |     if (options.ignoreCajaProperties) {
396 |       passes.add(ignoreCajaProperties);
397 |     }
398 | 
399 |     assertAllOneTimePasses(passes);
400 | 
401 |     if (options.smartNameRemoval || options.reportPath != null) {
402 |       passes.addAll(getCodeRemovingPasses());
403 |       passes.add(smartNamePass);
404 |     }
405 | 
406 |     // This needs to come after the inline constants pass, which is run within
407 |     // the code removing passes.
408 |     if (options.closurePass) {
409 |       passes.add(closureOptimizePrimitives);
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     // TODO(user): This forces a first crack at crossModuleCodeMotion
413 |     // before devirtualization. Once certain functions are devirtualized,
414 |     // it confuses crossModuleCodeMotion ability to recognized that
415 |     // it is recursive.
416 | 
417 |     // TODO(user): This is meant for a temporary quick win.
418 |     // In the future, we might want to improve our analysis in
419 |     // CrossModuleCodeMotion so we don't need to do this.
420 |     if (options.crossModuleCodeMotion) {
421 |       passes.add(crossModuleCodeMotion);
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     // Method devirtualization benefits from property disambiguiation so
425 |     // it should run after that pass but before passes that do
426 |     // optimizations based on global names (like cross module code motion
427 |     // and inline functions).  Smart Name Removal does better if run before
428 |     // this pass.
429 |     if (options.devirtualizePrototypeMethods) {
430 |       passes.add(devirtualizePrototypeMethods);
431 |     }
432 | 
433 |     if (options.customPasses != null) {
434 |       passes.add(getCustomPasses(
435 |           CustomPassExecutionTime.BEFORE_OPTIMIZATION_LOOP));
436 |     }
437 | 
438 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("beforeMainOptimizations"));
439 | 
440 |     if (options.specializeInitialModule) {
441 |       // When specializing the initial module, we want our fixups to be
442 |       // as lean as possible, so we run the entire optimization loop to a
443 |       // fixed point before specializing, then specialize, and then run the
444 |       // main optimization loop again.
445 | 
446 |       passes.addAll(getMainOptimizationLoop());
447 | 
448 |       if (options.crossModuleCodeMotion) {
449 |         passes.add(crossModuleCodeMotion);
450 |       }
451 | 
452 |       if (options.crossModuleMethodMotion) {
453 |         passes.add(crossModuleMethodMotion);
454 |       }
455 | 
456 |       passes.add(specializeInitialModule.makeOneTimePass());
457 |     }
458 | 
459 |     passes.addAll(getMainOptimizationLoop());
460 | 
461 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("beforeModuleMotion"));
462 | 
463 |     if (options.crossModuleCodeMotion) {
464 |       passes.add(crossModuleCodeMotion);
465 |     }
466 | 
467 |     if (options.crossModuleMethodMotion) {
468 |       passes.add(crossModuleMethodMotion);
469 |     }
470 | 
471 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("afterModuleMotion"));
472 | 
473 |     // Some optimizations belong outside the loop because running them more
474 |     // than once would either have no benefit or be incorrect.
475 |     if (options.customPasses != null) {
476 |       passes.add(getCustomPasses(
477 |           CustomPassExecutionTime.AFTER_OPTIMIZATION_LOOP));
478 |     }
479 | 
480 |     if (options.flowSensitiveInlineVariables) {
481 |       passes.add(flowSensitiveInlineVariables);
482 | 
483 |       // After inlining some of the variable uses, some variables are unused.
484 |       // Re-run remove unused vars to clean it up.
485 |       if (options.removeUnusedVars) {
486 |         passes.add(removeUnusedVars);
487 |       }
488 |     }
489 | 
490 |     // Running this pass again is required to have goog.events compile down to
491 |     // nothing when compiled on its own.
492 |     if (options.smartNameRemoval) {
493 |       passes.add(smartNamePass2);
494 |     }
495 | 
496 |     if (options.collapseAnonymousFunctions) {
497 |       passes.add(collapseAnonymousFunctions);
498 |     }
499 | 
500 |     // Move functions before extracting prototype member declarations.
501 |     if (options.moveFunctionDeclarations) {
502 |       passes.add(moveFunctionDeclarations);
503 |     }
504 | 
505 |     if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==
506 |         AnonymousFunctionNamingPolicy.MAPPED) {
507 |       passes.add(nameMappedAnonymousFunctions);
508 |     }
509 | 
510 |     // The mapped name anonymous function pass makes use of information that
511 |     // the extract prototype member declarations pass removes so the former
512 |     // happens before the latter.
513 |     //
514 |     // Extracting prototype properties screws up the heuristic renaming
515 |     // policies, so never run it when those policies are requested.
516 |     if (options.extractPrototypeMemberDeclarations &&
517 |         (options.propertyRenaming != PropertyRenamingPolicy.HEURISTIC &&
518 |          options.propertyRenaming !=
519 |             PropertyRenamingPolicy.AGGRESSIVE_HEURISTIC)) {
520 |       passes.add(extractPrototypeMemberDeclarations);
521 |     }
522 | 
523 |     if (options.ambiguateProperties &&
524 |         (options.propertyRenaming == PropertyRenamingPolicy.ALL_UNQUOTED)) {
525 |       passes.add(ambiguateProperties);
526 |     }
527 | 
528 |     if (options.propertyRenaming != PropertyRenamingPolicy.OFF) {
529 |       passes.add(renameProperties);
530 |     }
531 | 
532 |     // Reserve global names added to the "windows" object.
533 |     if (options.reserveRawExports) {
534 |       passes.add(gatherRawExports);
535 |     }
536 | 
537 |     // This comes after property renaming because quoted property names must
538 |     // not be renamed.
539 |     if (options.convertToDottedProperties) {
540 |       passes.add(convertToDottedProperties);
541 |     }
542 | 
543 |     // Property renaming must happen before this pass runs since this
544 |     // pass may convert dotted properties into quoted properties.  It
545 |     // is beneficial to run before alias strings, alias keywords and
546 |     // variable renaming.
547 |     if (options.rewriteFunctionExpressions) {
548 |       passes.add(rewriteFunctionExpressions);
549 |     }
550 | 
551 |     // This comes after converting quoted property accesses to dotted property
552 |     // accesses in order to avoid aliasing property names.
553 |     if (!options.aliasableStrings.isEmpty() || options.aliasAllStrings) {
554 |       passes.add(aliasStrings);
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     if (options.aliasExternals) {
558 |       passes.add(aliasExternals);
559 |     }
560 | 
561 |     if (options.aliasKeywords) {
562 |       passes.add(aliasKeywords);
563 |     }
564 | 
565 |     // Passes after this point can no longer depend on normalized AST
566 |     // assumptions.
567 |     passes.add(markUnnormalized);
568 | 
569 |     if (options.coalesceVariableNames) {
570 |       passes.add(coalesceVariableNames);
571 | 
572 |       // coalesceVariables creates identity assignments and more redundant code
573 |       // that can be removed, rerun the peephole optimizations to clean them
574 |       // up.
575 |       if (options.foldConstants) {
576 |         passes.add(peepholeOptimizations);
577 |       }
578 |     }
579 | 
580 |     if (options.collapseVariableDeclarations) {
581 |       passes.add(exploitAssign);
582 |       passes.add(collapseVariableDeclarations);
583 |     }
584 | 
585 |     // This pass works best after collapseVariableDeclarations.
586 |     passes.add(denormalize);
587 | 
588 |     if (options.instrumentationTemplate != null) {
589 |       passes.add(instrumentFunctions);
590 |     }
591 | 
592 |     if (options.variableRenaming != VariableRenamingPolicy.ALL) {
593 |       // If we're leaving some (or all) variables with their old names,
594 |       // then we need to undo any of the markers we added for distinguishing
595 |       // local variables ("$$1").
596 |       passes.add(invertContextualRenaming);
597 |     }
598 | 
599 |     if (options.variableRenaming != VariableRenamingPolicy.OFF) {
600 |       passes.add(renameVars);
601 |     }
602 | 
603 |     if (options.groupVariableDeclarations) {
604 |       passes.add(groupVariableDeclarations);
605 |     }
606 | 
607 |     // This pass should run after names stop changing.
608 |     if (options.processObjectPropertyString) {
609 |       passes.add(objectPropertyStringPostprocess);
610 |     }
611 | 
612 |     if (options.labelRenaming) {
613 |       passes.add(renameLabels);
614 |     }
615 | 
616 |     if (options.foldConstants) {
617 |       passes.add(latePeepholeOptimizations);
618 |     }
619 | 
620 |     if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==
621 |         AnonymousFunctionNamingPolicy.UNMAPPED) {
622 |       passes.add(nameUnmappedAnonymousFunctions);
623 |     }
624 | 
625 |     if (options.operaCompoundAssignFix) {
626 |       passes.add(operaCompoundAssignFix);
627 |     }
628 | 
629 |     // Safety checks
630 |     passes.add(sanityCheckAst);
631 |     passes.add(sanityCheckVars);
632 | 
633 |     return passes;
634 |   }

===== M1182 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.consume(List<PassFactory>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 113-134
Documentation:
/**
   * Add the passes generated by the given factories to the compile sequence.
   *
   * Automatically pulls multi-run passes into fixed point loops. If there
   * are 2 or more multi-run passes in a row, they will run together in
   * the same fixed point loop. If A and B are in the same fixed point loop,
   * the loop will continue to run both A and B until both are finished
   * making changes.
   *
   * Other than that, the PhaseOptimizer is free to tweak the order and
   * frequency of multi-run passes in a fixed-point loop.
   */
Method code:
113 |   void consume(List<PassFactory> factories) {
114 |     Loop currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
115 |     boolean isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
116 |     for (PassFactory factory : factories) {
117 |       if (factory.isOneTimePass()) {
118 |         if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
119 |           passes.add(currentLoop);
120 | 
121 |           currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
122 |           isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
123 |         }
124 |         addOneTimePass(factory);
125 |       } else {
126 |         currentLoop.addLoopedPass(factory);
127 |         isCurrentLoopPopulated = true;
128 |       }
129 |     }
130 | 
131 |     if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
132 |       passes.add(currentLoop);
133 |     }
134 |   }

===== M1190 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.optimizeSubtree(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 73-157
Documentation:
/**
   * Tries apply our various peephole minimizations on the passed in node.
   */
Method code:
73 |   @Override
74 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
75 |   public Node optimizeSubtree(Node node) {
76 |     switch(node.getType()) {
77 |       case Token.RETURN: {
78 |         Node result = tryRemoveRedundantExit(node);
79 |         if (result != node) {
80 |           return result;
81 |         }
82 |         result = tryReplaceExitWithBreak(node);
83 |         if (result != node) {
84 |           return result;
85 |         }
86 |         return tryReduceReturn(node);
87 |       }
88 | 
89 |       case Token.THROW: {
90 |         Node result = tryRemoveRedundantExit(node);
91 |         if (result != node) {
92 |           return result;
93 |         }
94 |         return tryReplaceExitWithBreak(node);
95 |       }
96 | 
97 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Maybe remove redundant BREAK and CONTINUE. Overlaps
98 |       // with MinimizeExitPoints.
99 | 
100 |       case Token.NOT:
101 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
102 |         return tryMinimizeNot(node);
103 | 
104 |       case Token.IF:
105 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
106 |         return tryMinimizeIf(node);
107 | 
108 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
109 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
110 |         return node;
111 | 
112 |       case Token.HOOK:
113 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
114 |         return node;
115 | 
116 |       case Token.WHILE:
117 |       case Token.DO:
118 |         tryMinimizeCondition(NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(node));
119 |         return node;
120 | 
121 |       case Token.FOR:
122 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(node)) {
123 |           tryMinimizeCondition(NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(node));
124 |         }
125 |         return node;
126 | 
127 | 
128 |       case Token.TRUE:
129 |       case Token.FALSE:
130 |         return reduceTrueFalse(node);
131 | 
132 |       case Token.NEW:
133 |         node = tryFoldStandardConstructors(node);
134 |         if (node.getType() != Token.CALL) {
135 |           return node;
136 |         }
137 |         // Fall through on purpose because tryFoldStandardConstructors() may
138 |         // convert a NEW node into a CALL node
139 |       case Token.CALL:
140 |         return tryFoldLiteralConstructor(node);
141 | 
142 |       case Token.COMMA:
143 |         return tryFoldComma(node);
144 | 
145 |       case Token.NAME:
146 |         return tryReplaceUndefined(node);
147 | 
148 |       case Token.BLOCK:
149 |         return tryReplaceIf(node);
150 | 
151 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
152 |         return tryMinimizeArrayLiteral(node);
153 | 
154 |       default:
155 |         return node; //Nothing changed
156 |     }
157 |   }

===== M1202 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.run()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 283-321
Documentation:
/**
   * Runs the Compiler and calls System.exit() with the exit status of the
   * compiler.
   */
Method code:
283 |   final public void run() {
284 |     int result = 0;
285 |     int runs = 1;
286 |     if (config.computePhaseOrdering) {
287 |       runs = NUM_RUNS_TO_DETERMINE_OPTIMAL_ORDER;
288 |       PhaseOptimizer.randomizeLoops();
289 |     }
290 |     try {
291 |       for (int i = 0; i < runs && result == 0; i++) {
292 |         runTimeStats.recordStartRun();
293 |         result = doRun();
294 |         runTimeStats.recordEndRun();
295 |       }
296 |     } catch (AbstractCommandLineRunner.FlagUsageException e) {
297 |       System.err.println(e.getMessage());
298 |       result = -1;
299 |     } catch (Throwable t) {
300 |       t.printStackTrace();
301 |       result = -2;
302 |     }
303 | 
304 |     if (config.computePhaseOrdering) {
305 |       runTimeStats.outputBestPhaseOrdering();
306 |     }
307 | 
308 |     try {
309 |       if (jsOutput instanceof Closeable) {
310 |         ((Closeable) jsOutput).close();
311 |       }
312 |     } catch (IOException e) {
313 |       throw Throwables.propagate(e);
314 |     }
315 | 
316 |     if (testMode) {
317 |       exitCodeReceiverForTesting.apply(result);
318 |     } else {
319 |       System.exit(result);
320 |     }
321 |   }
